The engine uses one generic state schema and operation framework: fields, electrons, muons and gluons are distinguished by property content, representations and permitted interactions, not by separate fundamental engines. A ray is the propagation form of these properties, not an additional entity class. Uniform representation must retain physical differences, including charges, spin, statistics and couplings.

```text
property definitions + symmetry representations → Node state → propagation and interaction → detector records → observer-dependent display
```

At each Node, separately implemented generic operations evaluate owned properties and arrived inputs under immutable family/coupling definitions. The engine schedules, transports and validates; NodeState stores bounded data rather than physical formulas or executable expressions. Ordinary evolution is deterministic, while only a Node whose Detector bit is set draws (no draw since 2026-09-18, section 5.4, point 14). A Recorder stores evidence and a Renderer presents it. The material-eye and full species-dynamics goals remain separate from supported profiles; a visualization does not establish agreement with nature. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says, there being no ray, thing, shadow or bit, no draw and no return; what this describes is an event, in transit or measured, handled by the Node's one computation, and a Node keeps nothing.)

# 4. Central statement

Universe24 investigates one discrete Node-and-Link world in which properties propagate in ray form and matter, particles and mass are intended to emerge as stable patterns. Ordinary dynamics are local and deterministic; the only draw is at a Node whose Detector bit is set (no draw since 2026-09-18, section 5.4, point 14), and there is no shared resource, no hidden ordinary access and no second material world. Symmetry representations constrain generic property operations. Every mechanism must specify local state, arrivals, operations, owners, output clocks and causal transfers. The aim is to derive and test effective behavior without inserting the desired macroscopic laws. A defined contract or configured experiment is not yet a complete derived theory of nature. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says, there being no ray, thing, shadow or bit, no draw and no return; what this describes is an event, in transit or measured, handled by the Node's one computation, and a Node keeps nothing.)

# 5. Reading map

## 5.1 Node state and ray form

**State.** The state that moves is the ray (family properties, phase, heading, the number of steps it has made since its event, the information of its last event and, if that was a Detector event, its bit); a Node holds nothing but the rays resident this interval and, under a declared binding coupling, a bounded bound group. Since 2026-09-18 (section 5.4, definitions and points 22, 25): a ray carries an amount, a phase, a heading and a bit, a thing its momentum too; a shadow carries no step count (point 3); a Node is its six Ports, two lanes each, one real slot and one shadow slot per owner on every lane, with the parked shadows of point 22, and holds nothing else. Superseded on 2026-09-18 (the evening) by the law of the shadow, section 5.4: there is no bit and no real slot; every ray is a shadow, and a Node holds its six Ports, the parked shadows and, where it is matter, held content, which moves by its momentum (reading (v) there; the orchestrator's reading). (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says, there being no ray, thing, shadow or bit, no draw and no return; what this describes is an event, in transit or measured, handled by the Node's one computation, and a Node keeps nothing.)

**Meetings.** At a meeting of rays the declared coupling between their families decides no interaction, a deterministic interaction with exact invariants and at most six events out, or a Detector interaction. There is no occupied channel and no capacity rule: rays cross, meet or bind by their declared couplings, and nothing is pushed back or made to wait for room. The occupied-channel displacement rule of the 2026-09-16 revision was deleted on 2026-09-17. Superseded for real rays on a lane on 2026-09-18 by point 25 of section 5.4 (model owner): a lane carries one real ray per interval, a thing steps into a lane only if the lane is free and otherwise keeps its heading and steps at the next Node, and two real rays of one family given one lane are one; nothing queues and nothing waits for room, the lane being a condition on the step and not a tie-break. Superseded on 2026-09-18 (the evening) by the law of the shadow, section 5.4: there is no real ray on a lane; a shadow that reaches held content is absorbed by the family's table, shadows of one number mix (point 24), and one real per lane and the merging of two reals are to be restated by round 8. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says, there being no ray, thing, shadow or bit, no draw and no return; what this describes is an event, in transit or measured, handled by the Node's one computation, and a Node keeps nothing.)

**Layers.** Event spacetime has layers: two events can happen at the same Node in the same interval in layers that do not communicate, because rays whose families have no declared coupling never meet; they cross as if the other were not there. A layer is a set of families that couple; a meeting exists only inside a layer.

## 5.2 Generic operations at a Node

Arrived input, one bounded local update per tick, a local proposal and atomic commit. Physical execution is at the Node; the separate generic operations package is code organization, not another physical place. Immutable family and coupling definitions own physical transformations and conserved readouts. The engine schedules, transports and validates, while NodeState contains bounded data and ownership, not physical formulas.

**The Node's law in five steps (model owner, 2026-09-17).** A Node knows nothing about electrons or stars; it is a switchboard with six Ports, six output clocks and a table to read. Each interval: (1) receive what arrived on the six Ports, with what is resident (a bound group); (2) apply its mark, if any: a Detector draws once per arrival, 0 returned and 1 continued; an external body absorbs into its sink and radiates by its amount; (3) meet by table, layer by layer: families with no declared coupling cross as if alone, families with one produce the outputs the table says, with exact invariants and the remainder placed by section 3.17; (4) stamp every output as a new event ray, with its event's Ports, shares and steps 0; (5) depart, each ray through its Port when that face's clock is ready. The two marks, the Detector and the external body, are the whole apparatus of a world: they are the only places where the board does something the tables do not say, and both are declarations in the initial file, never physics. Since 2026-09-18 (section 5.4): a Node has no output clocks and no departure waits for one (point 21), the one wait being the tick of point 23; a mark draws nothing, it absorbs a thing and returns a shadow (points 6, 14); a shadow meeting a thing is a push and a return (point 3), the shadows of one owner are mixed among the six Ports (point 24), and the tables of step (3) stand for a thing meeting a thing. Superseded on 2026-09-18 (the evening) by the law of the shadow, section 5.4: no thing meets a thing and nothing is returned; each interval the Node mixes the shadows that arrived (point 24), and where it holds content it absorbs the whole quanta by the family's table, holds or releases again, and releases in proportion to its content, the step of held content being a release toward the neighbour and an absorption there (readings (i) and (v) there). (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says, there being no ray, thing, shadow or bit, no draw and no return; what this describes is an event, in transit or measured, handled by the Node's one computation, and a Node keeps nothing.)

## 5.3 Couplings, fields and timing

Propagation, funded emission, remainders, six independent output clocks, no input delay and fixed neighboring Link transit H. Mass representation, rest phase and computation delay are distinct. A free ray never meets its own field because the field is faster and leaves ahead of it or away from it (section 3.5); newly output-delayed moving emitters remain unsupported until their composition is defined and tested. The strong-interaction long-residence and computation-field-emission idea is a research hypothesis, not proof of nuclear binding; weak conversion channels need their own explicit operators. Both, and polarization, are catalog entries on the same engine (section 3.26), scheduled after feature 10. The six output clocks and the computation delay are superseded on 2026-09-18 by section 5.4, points 21 and 23: every ray moves one Link per interval, and the one wait is the tick a thing pays per whole quantum it reads. Superseded again on 2026-09-18 (the evening) by the law of the shadow, section 5.4: the one wait reads the size and is paid by every quantum, held or in flight (reading (iii) there); the emission is held content's, every interval in proportion to its content, and whether its gravity and electricity are free and its light paid is what round 8 must show. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says, there being no ray, thing, shadow or bit, no draw and no return; what this describes is an event, in transit or measured, handled by the Node's one computation, and a Node keeps nothing.)

## 5.4 The Detector

**PASS and RETURN.** Only a Node whose Detector bit is set may draw, and replay never redraws or re-emits. 1 = PASS is the marked Node behaving as an ordinary Node for that arrival, the ray continuing on its line or interacting; 0 = RETURN is the same wave ray reversed on its line, unchanged, walking back the number of steps it has made since its event. One draw per arriving transfer, independently for up to six arrivals in one interval; every ray is a wave ray, so the kind of ray makes no difference. PASS/RETURN at a marked Node is the quantum measurement itself. Only PASS is a measurement; RETURN is none: nothing is measured, nothing is recorded as an outcome, the Node waits. The action distribution is not assumed to be 50/50. The return content is the arriving content unchanged; the return-content/phase laws, LOCK lifecycle, clock mapping and cancellation handling of the former shared resource lapsed with section 3.18. Superseded on 2026-09-18 by the law of the bit below: a mark draws nothing (point 14), it absorbs a thing and returns a shadow (point 6), and a return is a field, not a walk back by a step count (point 3). Superseded again on 2026-09-18 (the evening) by the law of the shadow (below): no thing and no shadow to tell apart, no PASS and no RETURN; a mark is held content with a table that counts, and a measurement is the absorption of a whole quantum there. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says, there being no ray, thing, shadow or bit, no draw and no return; what this describes is an event, in transit or measured, handled by the Node's one computation, and a Node keeps nothing.)

**A pair.** A returning ray retraces its own trajectory by its step count, reaches its birth interaction with certainty and there performs the inverse split of its share: it transmits what happened at the event, with its bit, to the same places the event sent to, the partner ray's line among them, so the partner's Detector is not missing it: the first Detector saw only its own value, and the second Detector receives that value on the ray that reaches it. With two Detectors, Alice's and Bob's, whichever returns first sends its value through the birth event and the other receives it; sometimes it is Alice's information, sometimes Bob's. To Alice and Bob the correlation feels as if it were decided at time zero, but nothing happened at time zero: the value was carried through the birth event in event spacetime, one Link per interval. Every Detector behaves the same: the second Detector draws its own bit on that arrival like on any other and reads nothing from the ray; the received value is information on the ray, not an input to the draw (superseded on 2026-09-17 for rays that carry a bit: see the next paragraph, a Detector reads the bit). The information of the last event, its Ports and shares, stays on the ray as a hidden variable: no Detector and no ordinary coupling reads it, it comes from no ordinary physics, and nothing on the board feels it. The Detector's bit is different since 2026-09-17: it is a visible property of the ray (next paragraph). (2026-09-18: for a shadow the return is a field and retraces nothing, point 3; what a mark that misses a thing does is point 6.) (2026-09-18, the evening, the law of the shadow, below: nothing retraces and nothing is carried through a birth event; Bell is two events of one field at two places, each born of the local field and the local setting, at most 2, reading (vii).) (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says, there being no ray, thing, shadow or bit, no draw and no return; what this describes is an event, in transit or measured, handled by the Node's one computation, and a Node keeps nothing.)

**The Detector's bit is a property of the ray (model owner, 2026-09-17).** Whoever meets a ray sees that it came from a Detector: the bit a marked Node set on a ray, 1 for PASS and 0 for RETURN, travels with the ray as a property like charge, visible to every meeting, to the record and to the rendering, and it propagates: the outputs of any event a marked ray takes part in carry it, so the descendants of a realized ray are known to be realized and the descendants of a transmission are known to carry a return; where the inputs of one event carry different bits, the declared coupling says which bit the outputs carry, and the default written in the catalog is that 1 outranks 0 and 0 outranks none. A coupling in the catalog may read the bit as it reads charge, so an apparatus that behaves differently for realized content is a catalog entry, not an engine mechanism (section 3.26). A Detector reads the bit (model owner, 2026-09-17, closing the open decision of that morning): a ray carrying 1 is already realized and passes a later Detector without a draw, as a measurement repeated in the same basis repeats its result; a ray carrying 0 is a transmission and is never drawn, it passes the marked Node and does what a transmission does; only a ray carrying no bit is drawn. How a marked Node meets each bit is its declared coupling in the catalog, with this as the default and the draw on every arrival, as before, the declarable alternative, so the rule is a table entry and not an engine mechanism (section 3.26); feature 2b implements both. This supersedes, for rays that carry a bit, the sentence of the pair paragraph that the second Detector draws on every arrival and reads nothing from the ray; the price of section 5.4 is to be re-derived under this rule by hypothesis 11 and experiment A13 before it is quoted again. The engine already carries the bit and stamps it on the outputs of the inverse split (features 1 to 4); the inheritance at every meeting, the catalog read and the visible mark in the record and the rendering are feature 2b, after feature 10. Under the law of the bit (2026-09-18) there is no ray without a bit and no draw: 1 is a thing, 0 its shadow, a mark absorbs the one and returns the other (points 6, 14), and a mark reads the bit, it does not draw on it. Superseded on 2026-09-18 (the evening) by the law of the shadow (below): there is no bit on any ray; a mark absorbs whole quanta by its table and counts them. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says, there being no ray, thing, shadow or bit, no draw and no return; what this describes is an event, in transit or measured, handled by the Node's one computation, and a Node keeps nothing.)

**A click is an absorption (model owner, 2026-09-18).** The field quantum a marked Node realizes ends there: on a draw of 1 the arriving field content is absorbed into the mark's exact counter, per family, booked on the audit as absorbed by marks with its momentum on the mark's line, and nothing of it spreads on; a photon is detected by being absorbed, and every measurement is paid (section 3.14). A draw of 0 stays the return of section 3.19, the ray sent back on its line unchanged, a perfect mirror; a ray carrying 1 still passes without a draw. Matter that draws 1 passes with the bit 1 as before, so the electron seen carries its mark; how a mark meets each family on a click, absorb or pass, is its declared coupling in the catalog, with absorb the default for a field family and pass the default for matter (section 3.26), so a screen that stops electrons and a counter that lets light through are catalog entries. The bit is therefore the mark of content that passed a Detector without being absorbed, and the field a Node re-releases never carries the bit of what was realized, because what was realized is no longer there to spread. Decided on the finding of the loop screen (E9, 2026-09-17): with the clicked quantum spreading on, its bit left the marks along the screen and back toward the source and the screen stopped clicking at tick 72, a screen that remembers; a screen that absorbs counts, and its count grows as an intensity, which the two-slit run A1 needs. Feature 2c implements it; E9 is repeated under it. The same day (points 6, 12 and 14): no draw and no field family; a mark absorbs a thing, or passes it if its declared coupling for the family says so, returns a shadow, and re-releases nothing. Under the law of the shadow (2026-09-18, the evening, below) this stands and is the whole of measurement: a click is the absorption of a whole quantum at the mark's held content; the return on a draw of 0 is gone, since nothing returns, and what a mark's table says per family is absorb, hold or release again. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says, there being no ray, thing, shadow or bit, no draw and no return; what this describes is an event, in transit or measured, handled by the Node's one computation, and a Node keeps nothing.)

**Definitions for the law of the bit (fixed terms, model owner, 2026-09-18; one name per thing, one thing per name).** *Node*: a point of the lattice; it is its six *Ports*, one per heading, and holds nothing else (point 22). *Link*: what joins two Nodes; it carries rays both ways. *Lane*: one direction of a Port, in or out; two per Port, twelve per Node; a lane carries per interval at most one real and one shadow per owner (point 25). *Interval*: one tick; every ray moves one Link per interval (point 21), a thing that waits excepted (point 23). *Ray*: content in motion on a line, with an amount, a phase, a heading and a bit, and, for a thing, a momentum. *Amount*: whole quanta; it is never negative and never destroyed. *Phase*: an angle on a circle of *N* steps; every ray advances it by its content / *K* per interval, light included, whose content is its amount (model owner, 2026-09-18, the evening: "the phase rotates like a ray"; so light's frequency is its amount, Planck's relation as a consequence); a shadow carries its owner's phase as it was at its release ("a release stamps": every release, the prefill's and the re-release at home alike, stamps the owner's phase of that interval, so a source with a clock makes a wave and a source without one a still field). *Heading*: the Port a ray leaves by. *Bit*: the one property that says what a ray is; its two values are *real* (1) and *shadow* (0). *Family*: the declared kind of a thing (electron, proton, light); it declares the charge of a thing of it, one whole number whatever the thing's content (point 16 as amended), its tables and its phase width *N* (points 12, 17, 19), and its quantum, of which every thing of it is a whole multiple (point 25). *Thing*: a real ray (an electron, a photon, a piece of a split); it has mass and a clock, one path, and its bit never changes at a meeting (points 9, 10). *Shadow*: a ray with bit 0, the field of a thing, of the same family as its thing (point 12); it carries its owner's identity, charge and content as a message, has no mass and no clock, and is absorbed where it reaches its owner (point 3). *Owner*: the thing a shadow belongs to; one number, given to a thing at its birth and carried by its shadows, the name of the coupling between a thing and its field (model owner, 2026-09-18: "a shadow and its source have the same label, and a shadow always has one label"). The shadow does nothing with it; the Node reads it in two places: shadows with one number are one field and mix as amplitudes, shadows with different numbers are different fields and are counted (point 24); a shadow that meets a real with its own number is home, absorbed and released again, and with any other number it pushes and is turned back (point 3). A shadow never knows whom to return to (model owner, 2026-09-18: "it is simply turned back at a meeting with a 1"); the number is not a route, a memory or a step count, it is a property like the sign of the charge. Why the number is needed (the orchestrator's elaboration, 2026-09-18): without it the mixing makes one wave of every source, so the force of two things at rest on a third ripples with the period N; the reflection of a thing's own field (four ninths of every share, back within two intervals) pushes the thing and its returns land on a stranger with a momentum proportional to the square of its content; and a return is absorbed by the first real it meets, so a push's recoil never reaches the source. What physics does with the same number: the self-field of a charge does not push it, and the fields of different sources add, they do not interfere. A merged thing (point 25) carries the numbers of all it merged. Under the emission of the same evening ("A thing emits"): every thing gets its number at its birth and stamps it on what it emits, and nothing inherits, so a thing born at an event, emitted or a piece of a split, has its own number and its own field from birth (a lamp's field pushes the light it emitted, a star's its own light), and the field of a thing that ended streams off the board; the guidance of a thing through two slits is then the ordinary push of its source's field (point 16), and home stays what point 3 says, a zero (the orchestrator's reading, adopted by the model owner, 2026-09-18, "closed"). *Shadow set*: all the shadows of one thing, its size proportional to the thing's content (point 18). *Standing set*: the shadows around a thing at rest, a steady state a formula can give (points 11, 13). *Content*: the amount of a thing; it is its mass and its clock (point 19). *Momentum*: a property of a thing, a vector it carries like its amount and its phase; every push adds to it, and when its component on an axis reaches the thing's content the thing steps to that axis and the momentum drops by that content (point 21). *Meeting*: two rays at one Node in one interval. *Event*: a change of trajectory at a Node that holds a real ray (point 13); a push is not one (point 15). *Push*: the field's arithmetic on a thing's momentum, what a thing takes from a shadow it meets (point 15). *Reading*: what a thing multiplies a shadow's message by, gravity by content and electricity by charge (point 16). *Return*: a ray turned back at a meeting, and only at a meeting (model owner, 2026-09-18: "returns are a meeting of a shadow with a real, or a real with a real"): a shadow meeting a thing is pushed on and turned back, the same shadow with its heading reversed and its momentum inverted, a field like any other from then on (point 3); a thing meeting a thing turns by the pair's table (point 4: steering by the phase difference within a family, point 17; the table of section 5.2 between families; a mirror is the table "reversed heading"). A mark is a thing with a table (point 22): it absorbs things and it is pushed by shadows and turns them back as any thing does, so a mark's return is a meeting like every other and carries the push inverted (point 6 as amended); the engine's exception, a mark returning a shadow with nothing carried, is retired (feature 16h). *Remainder*: a shadow parked at a Node, an amount below one quantum waiting to become one (section 3.17, point 22). *Mixing*: the Node's step on the shadows of one owner that arrive in an interval, the coherent sum shared among the six Ports (point 24); it is how a shadow spreads. *Steering*: two things of one family meeting, the share leaving each Port set by the table of their phase difference (section 5.2); the table is computed from *N* (point 17). *Wait*: the tick a thing pays for every whole quantum it reads (point 23). *Mark*: a Node whose Detector bit is set; it absorbs things into a resident thing, its counter, and returns shadows (points 6, 22). *Source*: a thing that spends its own content as things of another family by an emission table (point 22). *Prefill*: the shadows given with the board at the start; a thing does not emit them. *Books*: the ledger, kept per bit; things conserve amount, momentum and charge among themselves exactly, shadows are initial content (point 7). *K*: the content per phase step per interval, one for the world. *N*: the number of steps of the phase circle, the one input behind interference; one for the world, like *K*, declared once and 64 (model owner, 2026-09-18): a phase difference between any two rays, of one family or of two, is read on the one circle, so a width per family means nothing; at 64 steps the mixing's rounding costs nothing (DERIVATIONS.md round 4), and a wider circle adds no measured precision. The per-family `phase_bits` of the engine is retired in the cleanup. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: one name, event; a Node is its six Ports and keeps nothing, neither a share below one nor a count; no ray is excepted from point 21 by a wait, the exit of a Port being suspended by what is present in the interval.)

**Definitions for the law of the shadow (the orchestrator's draft from the model owner's words, 2026-09-18, flagged).** *Shadow*: the only ray ("No real and shadow. There is only shadow."): a whole quantum of a family in flight, moving one Link per interval and spreading by the Node's mixing (point 24) from the event that released it, its phase rotating by its content over *K*, light's content being its amount; it carries its family, its amount, its phase, its heading and its number, and it never disappears. *Event*: a whole quantum at a Node that holds content, where it is absorbed, held or released again by the family's table; from the event the shadows spread ("There are events, which are a whole quantum."). "Events are created only at a detector" and "what the eye sees is events", the model owner's words. *Held content*: matter: content held at a Node, the only place events happen; the external body and the mark's counter are its forms. It releases, absorbs, holds and releases again by its family's table, and it moves by its momentum. *Release*: what held content does every interval: it emits shadows in proportion to its content ("A thing emits"; "a real emits shadow forever"), each stamped with the holder's phase of that interval and its number. *Absorption*: the event: a whole quantum taken in at held content by the family's table. The push is an absorption: the quantum's momentum, with the family's sign, gravity's toward the source, times the holder's cross-section, its content for gravity and its whole charge for electricity (charge per thing, point 16 as amended), so the equivalence and Coulomb stay; there is no return and no home. *Re-release*: a whole quantum absorbed at held content and released again by the family's table, which is what a mirror is; it leaves with the holder's number and phase stamped. *Hold*: an absorption whose quantum stays as the holder's content; light, a paid family, is held, and its hold at a mark is a click (that gravity and electricity are free and light paid is what round 8 must show). *Step*: how held content moves: when a component of its momentum reaches its content (point 21), a release toward the neighbour and an absorption there; so nothing outruns the field, and light and matter share one c, the field's. *Wait*: the tick paid where the size of the field, the whole units of the coherent sum of the shadows at the Node, is read; paid by every quantum, held or in flight: a held content's release slows in a potential (the clock, the redshift), and a quantum of light in flight steps slower where the size is larger (an index, n = 1 + 2GM/r). *Number*: the label a release stamps on a quantum, the last emitter's; a quantum absorbed and released again carries the holder's number and phase, so coherence is the last emitter, with no inheritance and no orphan, and shadows of one number mix as one field (point 24). *Mark*: held content with a table that counts; the whole quanta it absorbs are its clicks and its counter is its content. *Lamp*: held content that releases light, a paid family, and spends its own content in doing so (the source of point 22 in this law's words). *Edge*: the boundary of an open board, standing for infinity as it does in physics ("an open board, then"); a shadow that leaves through it is booked as escaped and never comes back, since a shadow never disappears and nothing returns. *Books*: one line for held content and one for the shadows; the shadows' line carries the emission, the escaped and the absorbed, and momentum held, in flight and escaped is constant at every interval. *N*, *K*: as in the glossary above, one for the world each: *N* the steps of the phase circle, 64, and *K* the content per phase step per interval; every ray's phase, a shadow's included, rotates by its content over *K*. *ρ*: the rate of release, the shadows a held content emits per unit of content per interval (the orchestrator's name for the ratio of point 18 that "A thing emits" made a rate); its value is the model owner's (section 5.5). (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says: no thing or shadow, no register, remainder or parked share, no return; an event's exit is one of seven, by the shares and its momentum, its suspension is carried, its clock is the count of its self-creations, and matter is a measured event.)

**The law of the bit: the thing or its shadow (model owner, 2026-09-18).** Every ray carries one bit, and the bit says what the ray is: 1, the thing itself; 0, its shadow, the field. Superseded the evening of 2026-09-18 by the law of the shadow (the paragraph below): there is no thing ray, every ray is a shadow, and an event is a whole quantum at held content. Nothing else distinguishes rays (section 3.3: there is no light and no matter), and there is no ray without a bit. (1) Birth: what is born from content is a thing, a seeded ray, a ray emitted at an event, the pieces of a split; what is released as field is a shadow. Only a thing releases shadows; a shadow releases nothing, it only spreads. (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: nothing born is a thing; every ray is a shadow, released by held content every interval in proportion to its content, and an event is a whole quantum at held content, absorbed, held or released again.) (2) Motion: a thing moves whole on its line and turns by its momentum, which the pushes it takes build up; a shadow spreads by the declared table (section 3.5) (since point 24 of the same day: by the Node's mixing, nothing declared). (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: no ray moves whole on a line of its own; every ray spreads by the Node's mixing, and held content moves by its momentum, the step a release toward the neighbour and an absorption there, reading (v).) (3) A shadow meeting a thing: the thing takes the push the coupling declares; the shadow turns back with the opposite sign (model owner, 2026-09-18): it is the same shadow, its heading reversed and its momentum inverted, and from there it is a field like any other, it mixes at every Node (point 24), its phase is a shadow's phase, and it pushes whatever it meets with the opposite sign; wherever it reaches its owner it is absorbed. The meeting creates nothing: what the thing gained is carried away by the shadow inverted, and the books close at every interval with that momentum in flight (point 7). The recoil is global, delivered through the field, not by a message that finds its sender: the third law holds through the field, as it does in physics, and not pairwise. There is no return walk (model owner, 2026-09-18, superseding the chase of the same day): no step counter, no trace to follow, no chase; under point 24 what reached the thing is a sum of many paths and has no steps of its own, and the field alone holds every path, since it spits shares to the sides at every Node; a share of the inverted shadow that reaches the owner's Node is absorbed there, and the rest is the owner's field with the opposite sign wherever it is. A thing meeting its own shadow is the same rule with a round trip of length zero (model owner, 2026-09-18): the push and its return meet at one Node in one interval and sum to zero, so nothing changes and the shadow is absorbed and released again with the thing; "home" names that result, it is no rule of its own, and the engine computes the zero directly. (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: the push is an absorption of a whole quantum at held content, its momentum with the family's sign times the holder's cross-section; there is no return and no home, and the third law is the symmetry of the two fields, reading (ii).) (4) A thing meeting a thing: the declared tables of section 5.2 as before, binding, absorption, emission, split, corner. (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: a whole quantum at held content is absorbed, held or released again by the family's table; binding, split, corner and pair creation as tables: to be restated by round 8.) (5) A shadow meeting a shadow: the coherent sum of section 3.20, a phase that decides the heading (point 17), never an amount. (6) A marked Node: a thing that arrives is absorbed into the mark's counter and counted, with its momentum on the mark's line, or passes if the mark's declared coupling for its family says pass; a mark that misses a thing sends it back on its steps to its birth event by its declared table (point 14), and nothing is drawn. A thing has steps (model owner, 2026-09-18, on the consistency audit): it has one path and counts it, the real counts and the shadow does not, so this walk back stands, and the step counter that point 3 retires is the shadow's. A shadow that arrives is returned as at any thing: a mark counts nothing of a field. Amended (model owner, 2026-09-18, "returns are a meeting of a shadow with a real, or a real with a real"): the mark's resident thing is a thing with the mark's declared table, so a shadow that arrives meets it as it meets any thing, pushes it by that table (a screen takes the pressure of the field) and is turned back carrying the push inverted (point 3); the return with nothing carried that the engine made for a mark is no rule and is retired (feature 16h). The orchestrator's note, flagged: what a return carries decides whether a thing absorbing its own field at home (point 3) is moved sideways by the field's pattern or only along the line of the push; that reading is a derivation before A1 is repeated, not a rule of this point. (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: a mark is held content with a table that counts, a click the absorption of a whole quantum there; nothing walks back on steps and nothing is turned back, there being no return; the miss of an imperfect mark: to be restated by round 8.) (7) The books (section 3.15): things conserve amount, momentum and charge exactly among themselves, with no source line; shadows are free, given with the board as initial content and never sourced; a source is a thing that spends its content (point 22), so no line is sourced at all, and the books are kept per bit (superseded the same evening: "A thing emits", below; the books of the shadows then carry an emission line and a sink line). (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: one line for held content and one for the shadows, with the emission, the escaped and the absorbed, and momentum held, in flight and escaped constant; the glossary of the law of the shadow, *Books*.) (8) What is not known: at a Node, before a ray arrives, whether a 0 or a 1 comes; that is the whole uncertainty of the model, and nothing else is random. (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: there is no 0 or 1 to come; what is not known before an arrival is whether a whole quantum is made at this Node, the distribution over many being the amount's, Born by counting, reading (vi); the orchestrator's reading.) (9) The bit also says what has mass and what costs computation (model owner, 2026-09-18): a thing's content is mass and it advances a clock (at content / K per interval, point 19); it delays nothing, since every ray moves one Link per interval (point 21; "delays a Node" struck on 2026-09-18); a shadow has no mass and no clock, and moves at the causal speed. The shadow is free in the books and free in time. Amended (model owner, 2026-09-18, the evening): light's phase rotates like every ray's, by its content over K, its content being its amount; "light carries the phase of what emitted it" names the phase it starts with, not a phase that stands still. A shadow still has no clock: its phase is its owner's at its release, stamped at every release (the glossary, *Phase*); feature 16h carries both into the engine, where the prefill released at phase 0 and a re-release kept the old phase (the A1 and E9 lanes of the same day found only the phases 0 and 32 in the field). (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: mass is held content and its clock is its release, which slows in a potential, reading (iii); every ray's phase rotates by its content over K, a shadow's included, and every quantum pays the wait; what costs computation: to be restated by round 8.) (10) A thing has one path and only one (model owner, 2026-09-18): at every meeting with a shadow the declared coupling decides where it goes next, and nothing else does; the bit never changes at a meeting, a thing stays a thing with its mass, its clock and its shadows however many pushes it has taken, and only a mark takes it off the board. Everything that is not that one path is shadow, unrealized. What an observer does not know between two marks is the observer's, not the ray's: the board holds the thing at one Node, exactly, always. (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: nothing walks a path of its own; between events there are only shadows, and "what the eye sees is events".) (11) The computation is constant (model owner, 2026-09-18): computation is mass, and mass is the content of things, which is conserved among them; so the world's computation per interval is the sum of its things' content, the same whatever splits, joins or turns, and it falls only when a mark absorbs a thing, whose content leaves the board for a record. Shadows cost no computation however many circulate: the engine's work is the things, and the field is a standing set that a formula can give. (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: to be restated by round 8.) (12) A shadow is a ray of the same family as its thing, with the bit 0 (model owner, 2026-09-18): there is no field family. An electron's shadow is an electron with 0, a proton's a proton with 0; it carries the family's charge sign, its phase and its owner, and none of the family's rest rate or mass, because 0 means it does not exist as a thing and exists only as a message. Everything that was declared of a field family, the spread table, the size of the shadow set, the coupling that reads it, is declared of the family and read by the bit. Light emitted at an event is a thing of the light family; a shadow has no shadow. (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: every ray is a shadow, a quantum of its family in flight, light among them, with no thing to belong to; its number is its last emitter's, reading (iv); the field family stays retired.) (13) Events happen only at Nodes that hold a 1 (model owner, 2026-09-18): a shadow meeting a shadow is a sum of phases and no event, a shadow meeting a thing is a push on the thing's momentum at the thing's Node (not an event by itself, point 15), a mark returning a shadow makes no event and a mark absorbing a thing makes one. The board therefore has two layers: the Nodes that hold a thing, cycled in full, and every other Node, which holds only shadows and is computed as one step, or by a formula at a standing field; the dense mode of the engine is that second layer. (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: events happen only at Nodes that hold content, where a whole quantum is absorbed, held or released again; the two layers are the Nodes with held content and the rest, which carry only shadows; the orchestrator's reading.) (14) There is no lottery (model owner, 2026-09-18): what a mark "draws" is whether a 0 or a 1 arrived, and that was decided at the ray's birth and along its one path, never at the mark. The mark throws nothing; it only did not know in advance. A mark that misses a thing is an imperfect instrument, declared as a table like a mirror or a splitter (it passes or returns one arrival in d), not a lottery; the draw of section 3.19 and the ticket seed are retired, and the only randomness in the model is the observer's ignorance of what arrives (point 8). (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: still no lottery, but nothing is decided at a birth or along a path; "an event is created when measuring", a whole quantum changing state at a Node, and what is not known before is point 8 as restated; the imperfect mark's table: to be restated by round 8.) (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says: no thing or shadow, no register, remainder or parked share, no return; an event's exit is one of seven, by the shares and its momentum, its suspension is carried, its clock is the count of its self-creations, and matter is a measured event.)

**Every action is a message that returns (model owner, 2026-09-18).** The general form of the law of the bit: a thing tells the world where it is, and that is its shadow; every effect the message has is paid by the same message returning to what sent it; nothing in the world changes without the message coming back to its sender. In the language of physics, a force is the round trip of a quantum, and conservation of momentum and energy is the statement that the round trip closes. In the language of information, the field is a transmission and not a substance: transmitting costs nothing, receiving (realization) costs, and the sender is told the exact price. In the language of arithmetic, every meeting is a pair of opposite steps on one line whose sum is zero, and the world is a sum of such pairs, which is why the books close at every Node and every interval. The distinction that holds all of it: 1 is what there is, 0 is what is said; what is said returns, what there is stays. (Point 3 as amended the same day: the message returns as a field, wherever it reaches its sender, and by no walk.) Superseded on 2026-09-18 (the evening) by the law of the shadow (below): the message does not return to its sender; every effect is an absorption at held content, paid in the books by the symmetry of the two fields, and "what is said" is all there is on the board, events being where it is absorbed (reading (ii) there; the orchestrator's reading). (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says: no thing or shadow, no register, remainder or parked share, no return; an event's exit is one of seven, by the shares and its momentum, its suspension is carried, its clock is the count of its self-creations, and matter is a measured event.)

**A meeting is reported to its owner (model owner, 2026-09-18).** When a shadow meets a thing, the owner of the shadow is told: the shadow's return is the report. It carries the amount that was read with the opposite sign, it travels as a field at the causal speed (point 3 as amended the same day: no walk, no trace), and its owner takes the recoil as it reaches it. So every push in the world is reported and no push goes unreported; between the push and the report the momentum is in flight in the shadow, and Newton's third law holds through the field, globally, exactly in the books at every interval. What is new in this against physics, as the orchestrator reads it (an elaboration, not a decision): physics has the reply only as an interpretation, the Wheeler-Feynman absorber (1945) and Cramer's transaction (1986), where the absorber answers with an advanced wave that runs backward in time, and the field itself is held to keep momentum of its own; here the reply runs forward, locally, as a field of one named owner, and it is not an interpretation but the mechanism of conservation: the books of things close because every message comes back. What follows and is measurable: the field holds no momentum of its own for good, every shadow comes home and only a thing (light among them) carries momentum away; the recoil of a source arrives late by the round trip and through the field, which A5s Run 2 reads at the source. Superseded on 2026-09-18 (the evening) by the law of the shadow (below): no meeting is reported and no recoil comes home; the third law is the symmetry of the two fields, what escapes to the open edge carrying equal and opposite momenta (reading (ii) there), and the field carries momentum for good, held, in flight and escaped being constant. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says: no thing or shadow, no register, remainder or parked share, no return; an event's exit is one of seven, by the shares and its momentum, its suspension is carried, its clock is the count of its self-creations, and matter is a measured event.)

**(15) A push is not an event (model owner, 2026-09-18).** A push is the field's arithmetic on a thing's momentum: the shadows that reach a thing add their momentum to it and turn back, and that is one operation over the whole board, or a formula at a standing field, and it costs nothing. An event is a change of trajectory (section 3.3): the step a thing makes when the momentum it carries has accumulated a whole amount on an axis, and a thing meeting a thing. So inside matter nothing is computed but the momenta: a glass of water is loops far apart on the lattice with shadows between them, its molecules are not colliding, they are pushed by each other's field as one sum, a thing at rest in a symmetric field has a push of zero and nothing runs, and events happen only when a thing meets a thing (light from outside absorbed, a group that breaks, an electron that jumps). The computation of the world is its things' clocks (point 11), never its interactions; the interactions are the field, and the field is free. (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: a push is an absorption, and an absorption is an event, a whole quantum at held content; the momentum absorbed is the quantum's with the family's sign times the holder's cross-section, and the step is a release toward the neighbour and an absorption there, readings (ii) and (v); nothing turns back.) (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says: no thing or shadow, no register, remainder or parked share, no return; an event's exit is one of seven, by the shares and its momentum, its suspension is carried, its clock is the count of its self-creations, and matter is a measured event.)

**(16) How a thing reads a shadow is the coupling (model owner, 2026-09-18).** The table of a shadow meeting a thing has, per pair of families, one rule: what the thing multiplies the message by. A shadow of a mass is read times the thing's content, so the push is proportional to the mass of what is pushed and the acceleration is not: every thing falls alike, which is the equivalence of gravity, and the mass of the pushed thing cancels as in Newton. For electricity the same shadow is read times its owner's charge over its owner's content, times the thing's charge with its sign, so the push is the product of the two charges and does not depend on the mass of what is pushed (the worked example: an electron of content 32 and charge −1 and a proton of content 64 and charge +1 push each other by equal and opposite amounts under both readings). The shadow is the same in both, the message is "how much and from where", and the two forces differ only in how a thing reads it (section 3.13). Until this rule, the momentum table gave the same push to every thing (A6: light and a slow ray took the same register), which is right for charge and wrong for gravity. Amended (model owner, 2026-09-18, "charge per thing"): the charge of a thing is one declared number of its family, whatever its content (an electron −1, a proton +1, a body its own declared charge), never a charge per quantum; it is what a thing multiplies an electric message by, whole; it adds when two things of one family become one ray (point 25) and every table conserves it. The source's charge over its content stays in the reading, since a shadow set is proportional to its thing's content (point 18): that quotient is the field's charge per quantum, and it is the message a shadow carries. Why (the orchestrator's reasons, adopted): every electron carries one e and every proton one e at 1836 times the mass, so charge does not go with mass; charge is conserved where content changes (binding, speed, decay); the rule itself says the electric push does not depend on the mass of what is pushed, and a charge per quantum is a charge proportional to mass (the cleanup measured it: a thing of 1000 quanta pushed a thousandfold, faster than a ray); and charge comes in whole units because it is a property of the thing. Implemented as feature 16g (charge-per-thing-v1). (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: the reading is the holder's cross-section on the whole quantum it absorbs, its content for gravity and its whole charge for electricity, so the equivalence and Coulomb stay, reading (ii); the charge per thing is the charge of the held content.) (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says: no thing or shadow, no register, remainder or parked share, no return; an event's exit is one of seven, by the shares and its momentum, its suspension is carried, its clock is the count of its self-creations, and matter is a measured event.)

**(17) A shadow spreads by its phase (model owner, 2026-09-18).** A fixed split table spreads a shadow like heat: the derivation of the split operator (DERIVATIONS.md) shows that every non-negative split gives a diffusion equation, a front at √t, and never a wave; so the field of a thing reached distance r after r² intervals, and light in shadow form could not go straight. From now the spread reads the phase: at a Node the shares of one owner that meet combine by the coherence rule (point 5, the amount unchanged), and the phase difference between the directions they came from steers where the combined content leaves, by the same steering table section 3.3 declares for the Born rule; shares arriving in one phase from behind continue forward, shares of differing phase are sent apart. The amount is exact and conserved as before; only the headings depend on the phase. This is Huygens' principle with phase, the field's front moves at the causal speed, interference of shadows is real, and point 5 reads "a shadow meeting a shadow is a sum of phases that decides the heading, never an amount". The split table of section 3.5 stays as the rule for shares that meet no other share of their owner (superseded for shadows the same day by point 24: the Node mixes the six, and the table serves the steering of two things of one family). The steering table is not a declared input (model owner, 2026-09-18, superseding "declared, not derived" in section 3.3): it is the integer geometry of the phase circle, computed once from the family's phase width N, the share that continues at a difference of d steps being the squared length of the sum of two unit phases d steps apart, cos²(πd/N), taken in N-ths and rounded to the nearest whole, the remainder owned by the Node as section 3.17 requires; at eight steps it is [8, 7, 4, 1, 0, 1, 4, 7]. No world declares its numbers and no catalog entry names them: the catalog declares which pairs steer, and the width N declares the table. The same table serves every steering, the shadows' spread and two things of one family that meet, and the only input behind interference is N. What this changes in the runs: t₉₀ ∝ r instead of r², the far field a front and not a haze; A5s, A6 and E11 are repeated under it after feature 16. (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: every ray spreads by the Node's mixing, point 24, with the table from N; the steering of two things of one family: to be restated by round 8.) (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says: no thing or shadow, no register, remainder or parked share, no return; an event's exit is one of seven, by the shares and its momentum, its suspension is carried, its clock is the count of its self-creations, and matter is a measured event.)

**(18) One shadow set per thing, read twice (model owner, 2026-09-18; restated the same day on the architecture review).** A thing has one shadow set, of size proportional to its content, and every shadow of it carries its owner's identity, charge and content. Gravity and electricity are the same shadows read twice by point 16: times the thing's content for gravity, times the owner's charge over its content and the thing's charge for electricity; so a neutral thing has shadows and gravity and no electric push, Coulomb's law is the product of the charges and Newton's the product of the masses, and there is no declared X for charge and no mass field family: the catalog's `mass_field` is retired. The derivation of Coulomb's law that gave a sum of fluxes (DERIVATIONS.md) assumed one reading of the shadow's amount alone; the owner's charge over its content on the shadow is what turns the sum into the product. (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: the set is a stream, released every interval in proportion to the held content, "A thing emits", and read at absorption by the holder's content or whole charge, reading (ii); the identity a shadow carries is its last emitter's number, reading (iv).) (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says: no thing or shadow, no register, remainder or parked share, no return; an event's exit is one of seven, by the shares and its momentum, its suspension is carried, its clock is the count of its self-creations, and matter is a measured event.)

**(19) A thing's clock is its content (model owner, 2026-09-18).** The rest rate is no longer declared per family: a thing at rest advances its phase by content / K steps per interval, with one universal K for every family, so mass, clock and computation are one number and point 11 is a literal count, the world's computation per interval being the sum of its things' phase steps. What moves at the causal speed has no rest and no clock, as before (light and shadows carry the phase of what emitted or released them). Consequences: a photon emitted at a transition carries the emitter's change of content and beats at the emitter's change of rate, so E = hν is conservation of amount together with this rule and h is K in lattice units; every thing with a clock surrounds itself with a phase pattern of wavelength K·c / content in its shadows, the Compton length, a length scale the model did not have; and a family declares only its charge and its tables. The phase steps per interval must stay below half the phase circle, so K and N together bound the content one Node may hold. The derivation of what follows for loops and levels is DERIVATIONS.md's; the value of K is the model owner's (section 5.5). (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: every ray rotates its phase by its content over K, light's content its amount, "the phase rotates like a ray", and a release stamps the holder's phase and number, reading (iv); the clock of held content is its release, which slows in a potential, reading (iii); K stays one for the world.) (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says: no thing or shadow, no register, remainder or parked share, no return; an event's exit is one of seven, by the shares and its momentum, its suspension is carried, its clock is the count of its self-creations, and matter is a measured event.)

**(20) A decay is a table, not a lottery (model owner, 2026-09-18).** The draw of feature 13 is retired with point 14: a bound group breaks when a declared condition on its own state is met (its content, its phase pattern, the number of its periods), a table of the catalog like every other meeting of things, deterministic, and the outputs are things. Whether a population of identical groups then decays by the exponential law is a question of their histories and their phases at birth, not of a die, and experiment A9 measures it. (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: to be restated by round 8.) (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says: no thing or shadow, no register, remainder or parked share, no return; an event's exit is one of seven, by the shares and its momentum, its suspension is carried, its clock is the count of its self-creations, and matter is a measured event.)

**(21) The speed of a thing (model owner, 2026-09-18).** Every ray moves one Link per interval, always; the momentum a thing carries sets the direction it takes next and never its speed, its step being the change of heading when a whole amount has accumulated on an axis (point 15); a bound group moves as a whole only by its loop, the corners shifting, and is slow because its period is long (section 3.4). This is the one rule of the engine, with the one exception of point 23 (the interval in which a thing reads a whole quantum); the sentences of section 3.28 on an output clock that delays departures describe the interim form and are superseded. (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: every shadow moves one Link per interval, and held content moves by its momentum as here, the step a release toward the neighbour and an absorption there, so nothing outruns the field and light and matter share one c, reading (v); the exception of point 23 is paid by every quantum, reading (iii).) (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says: no thing or shadow, no register, remainder or parked share, no return; an event's exit is one of seven, by the shares and its momentum, its suspension is carried, its clock is the count of its self-creations, and matter is a measured event.)

**(22) A Node is its six Ports; everything else is a ray (model owner, 2026-09-18).** Every store the model kept on a Node or beside the rays becomes a ray with a property: what was the remainder register is a shadow parked at the Node, a shadow like any other whose amount is below one quantum (in units of the split's denominator), so it combines, steers and returns as shadows do and the books count it as shadow content; what was the trace register is retired (2026-09-18): a return is a field and follows nothing, so a Node remembers no departure; a mark's counter is a thing resident at the mark, and a click is an absorption into matter like every absorption, the screen being matter and its count being content; a source is a thing that turns its own content into things of another family by an emission table, so nothing is injected and the sourced line vanishes, a lamp burns content; and the apparatus, mirrors, splitters, polarizers, walls, are things with declared tables. What remains of a Node is its Ports; what remains of the books is one ledger over rays per bit, with escape at the board's edge the only loss. The momentum stays what it is, a property of the thing, and the word register leaves the model with the stores it named. (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: the mark's counter, the source and the apparatus are held content with tables, matter's forms, reading (i); the parked remainder makes a whole quantum where the parts reach one, one absorption at one Node, reading (vi); the rest stands.) (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: there is no parked remainder either, not as a register and not as a shadow below one; what does not reach a whole for a side does not go to that side, a single event leaves whole to one side by the conservations of the cell, and a Node keeps nothing at all.)

**(23) A thing pays a tick for every whole quantum it reads (model owner, 2026-09-18).** A push is a meeting, a meeting is computation, and computation takes an interval: a shadow pays nothing, having no clock, but the thing that reads a whole quantum of shadow does not step and does not advance its phase in that interval. The world's computation stays the things' content (point 11); the wait is the thing's own time. What follows without a further rule: a clock near a mass runs slow, since more quanta reach it per interval, which is gravitational time dilation in integers and local; and light passing a mass waits more on its near side, so its front turns, which is the half of the bending that Einstein's 4GM/(c²b) has beyond Newton's. Point 21 reads "every ray moves one Link per interval, except the interval in which a thing reads a whole quantum". Amended the same evening (model owner, 2026-09-18, "do it like Einstein"): what a thing reads for its wait is the size of the field at its Node, the coherent sum's whole units, not the count of quanta; see "The wait reads the amplitude" below. How many intervals per quantum is the model's constant to measure (one is the natural value; the derivation of DERIVATIONS.md gives 11/9 for Einstein's coefficient exactly), read from A6 repeated and from a clock beside a mass; the derivation of the two consequences is the mathematician's before the rule is implemented (feature 16b carries it once derived). (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: the wait reads the size and is paid by every quantum, held or in flight; a held content's release slows in a potential, and a quantum of light in flight steps slower where the size is larger, an index n = 1 + 2GM/r, the bending and the Shapiro delay whole, reading (iii).) (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: the wait is the suspension of an exit, per Port, one rule for every family, derived in the interval from the events present at the Node and read by the tables without names; a Node counts nothing.)

**The wait reads the amplitude, a coupling to derive (model owner, 2026-09-18).** Derivations round 5 found that under the mixed field (point 24) a thing reads the field's flux, which falls as 1/r², while the field's amplitude, the size of the coherent sum the Node already forms for the mixing, falls as 1/r; no rule of the law reads the amplitude, so the wait of point 23 slows a clock by GM/r² and not by GM/r in every option and for every w, light's image bends by GM/b, and no choice of the shadow's wait changes that. The model owner's direction: what a thing reads is a coupling (point 16), and the coupling names which face of the field each reading takes: the push reads the amount (the count of quanta, the intensity, Newton's 1/r²), the wait reads the size of the coherent sum at the thing's Node (the amplitude, the potential, 1/r), each multiplied by the content as point 16 says; both numbers exist at the Node and no formula is written. This is physics' own division: what counts and pushes is the intensity, what interferes and sets time is the amplitude. Derived first (round 6: the four gravitational tests, the bending's two halves, Shapiro's logarithm, the books), implemented as the declared option `wait_reads` (feature 16f). Made the law the same evening (model owner, 2026-09-18: "do it like Einstein, that is more plausible"): the wait reads the size, the whole units of the coherent sum of the shadows of each source at the thing's Node, the sources added; the push reads the amount. Point 23 reads with this, the option is retired (feature 16h: the size is the only reading), and A6 repeated on the open board with the emission's fixed point measures the numbers, not the choice: one coefficient w for the clock and the redshift (round 6: w = 1.861√(Gm)), and whether the bending and the Shapiro delay come out whole or half. Under the law of the shadow (2026-09-18, the evening, below) the size is read by every quantum, held or in flight, and not by things alone (reading (iii) there). (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says: no thing or shadow, no register, remainder or parked share, no return; an event's exit is one of seven, by the shares and its momentum, its suspension is carried, its clock is the count of its self-creations, and matter is a measured event.)

**The board of a run is closed, and K is a constant (model owner, 2026-09-18).** A thing does not emit; its shadows are given with the board and circulate. On an open board they leave: the A6 lane found that the prefilled field of a thing at rest passes, parks as residue and escapes, so the field is transient and the force between two things at rest decays, which is no physics. The model owner's decision: the confrontation runs are made on a closed board (a periodic boundary), where every shadow comes around, what reaches its owner is absorbed and released again, and the field of a thing at rest is a steady circulation, not a passing wave; what that circulation gives at every radius is what the runs measure. K stays a constant of the world, not a background to be spread (HYPOTHESES.md 18 is recorded and not pursued). Only closed worlds are tested (model owner, 2026-09-18, "only closed worlds are tested"): a run series carries no open-board controls; what an open board does (the escape of the field, the decay of the force) was measured once by the A6 lane and is recorded, and every world of a confrontation run is periodic. The same evening the E11 lane measured the closed board with one thing at rest: no fixed point and no cycle, the field a train that circles the board and reconverges, the push on a probe without a law (see "A thing emits", below); and the same evening the model owner reversed this paragraph with point 7: a thing emits, a shadow never disappears, and the board of a run is open, its edge the infinity of physics ("A thing emits", below). The law of the shadow (the same evening, below) reads with the open board: the edge is infinity, what leaves is escaped, and a shadow never disappears. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says: no thing or shadow, no register, remainder or parked share, no return; an event's exit is one of seven, by the shares and its momentum, its suspension is carried, its clock is the count of its self-creations, and matter is a measured event.)

**The shadow's wait, a declared option to confront (model owner, 2026-09-18).** Whether the shadow is affected by the wait of point 23 is open: the law says it pays nothing (points 9 and 23), the missing time half of the bending (DERIVATIONS.md round 3) says the field near a mass may have to slow, and round 5 derives the three options. Until the runs decide, a world may declare `shadow_wait`, absent by default (the law as it stands), with a count of intervals per whole quantum and one of two readings: `thing`, a shadow that is read by a thing owes the wait before it leaves, or `field`, a shadow owes the wait for every whole quantum of another owner's shadows at the Node it crosses (an index of the field). It is an experiment's knob, not a rule of the law; A6 repeated runs the three, and what they give with round 5's coefficients is the model owner's decision. Implemented as feature 16e. Under the law of the shadow (2026-09-18, the evening, below, reading (iii)) the wait is paid by every quantum, in flight or held, reading the size, which closes this option; the option and its two readings describe the old engine, kept beside feature 20 until the new one has repeated every confrontation. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says: no thing or shadow, no register, remainder or parked share, no return; an event's exit is one of seven, by the shares and its momentum, its suspension is carried, its clock is the count of its self-creations, and matter is a measured event.)

**(24) The Node mixes the six (model owner, 2026-09-18).** A shadow does not choose its next heading alone, and not in a pair: at every Node, the shadows of one owner that arrive in an interval are one coherent sum (the amount as size, the phase as angle, on the phase circle of N steps), and each of the six Ports sends out one third of that sum less the arrival that came in through it, which is sent back. The amounts leaving are the total amount arriving, shared among the six Ports by the squared sizes of their six sums, in whole quanta with the remainders owned by the Node (section 3.17), and each leaving share carries the phase of its sum. Nothing here is declared: the third and the minus are what six equal Ports and exact conservation allow, and N is the family's phase width. This supersedes the split table of section 3.5 and the pairwise steering of point 17 for shadows (point 2 now reads: a shadow spreads by the Node's mixing); the tables of section 5.2 for a thing meeting a thing stand, and the return of point 3 stays a walk back on the trace, never a spread (superseded the same day by point 3 as amended: the return is a field and mixes like any shadow). What follows: a lone shadow mostly turns back (four ninths, one ninth on each other heading), a set in one phase moves as a wave because the turned-back parts of neighbours cancel, so the field is a collective and not a stream; the front is sharp, fills every direction and interferes, and the continuum limit is the wave equation on the lattice (the transmission-line matrix of Johns and Beurle, 1971, whose node is this one). The sign a wave needs is the heading, a returning shadow being the minus, while the amount stays non-negative and conserved. The model owner's reading: the flux a thing reads is the signed sum of the shadows at its Node, outgoing less returning, so a measurement sees the thing and its shadows together, and a cancellation is a zero flux with the amounts passing through, nothing destroyed. Declared to be measured, not assumed: the speed of the front in the bulk (this mesh gives about 1/√3 of a Link per interval at long wavelengths, while a thing of light moves one), the far field of a thing at rest (1/r² at every angle), and what stays standing beside a thing (the standing set of point 11). Implemented as feature 16c; derived in DERIVATIONS.md round 4 in parallel, the runs after 16 confronting both. (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: this is the motion of every ray on the board, "the shadow spreads like a ray from the event"; what held content reads of it is an absorption, reading (ii), and the size for its wait, reading (iii).) (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: the sides' shares stand as the Node's computation from the vectors, but only wholes leave and the Node owns no remainder; a lone event does not turn back in parts, it leaves whole to one side by the conservations of the cell, so a lone stream is a beam and the wave is the crowd; the front's speed and the far field are to be measured under this reading as they were under the old.)

**(25) A Port is two lanes, and a lane carries one real and one shadow per owner (model owner, 2026-09-18).** A Link joins two Nodes and carries rays both ways, so every Port has two lanes, in and out, and a Node has twelve. In one interval a lane carries at most one real ray and at most one shadow of each owner: a thing is a ray with an amount, so no intensity is limited, only the count of separate things on one Link in one direction; shadows of different owners share a lane because the fields of two things reach a third through one Port and each keeps its owner's number, the field it mixes with and the home that absorbs it (point 3; a shadow knows no route, it is turned back at a meeting with a real), and shadows of one owner on one lane are one sum (point 24). The state of a Node is therefore bounded and fixed before the run: six Ports, two lanes each, one real slot and one shadow slot per owner on every lane, plus the parked shadows and traces of point 22 (the traces retired the same day, point 22); the engine holds slots, not lists, and the dense layer's owner layers are this rule already. There is no queue and no wait in this (model owner, 2026-09-18): the lane is a condition on the step, not a tie-break. A thing steps into a lane only if the lane is free; otherwise it keeps its heading, its momentum stays accumulated, and it steps at the next Node; every thing moves one Link every interval as before. Two things never arrive on one lane, since no Port ever sends two, and a declared board with two rays on one lane is refused; a table of section 5.2 gives its outputs distinct lanes. A source emits and its thing leaves and arrives, always; no condition of a free lane stands on a source. When two real rays of one family are given one lane in one interval (a thing born there, emitted, returned by a mark or put out by a table, while another passes), they are one real ray (model owner, 2026-09-18): every thing of a family is a whole multiple of the family's quantum, so the amounts add, the phase is the coherent sum's (the amount never cancels, point 5), momentum and charge add exactly, and the owners are kept as a set so the shadows of both come home; what a thing whose content exceeds the family's bound then does is the family's decay table (point 20), and light has no bound, so light on one lane is one ray of many quanta. Two real rays of different families on one lane are a meeting decided by the table of section 5.2 for the pair, and a world that gives such a pair no table is refused. The only wait in the world is the clock's (point 23), counted per Link: a thing that owes a tick does not cross its Link in that interval, and nothing else ever holds a ray. Implemented as feature 18, after 16b and 16c. (Under the law of the shadow, 2026-09-18, the evening: there is no real slot, every ray being a shadow, and shadows share a lane per number, the last emitter's, reading (iv); one real per lane and the merging of two reals into one: to be restated by round 8.) (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says: no thing or shadow, no register, remainder or parked share, no return; an event's exit is one of seven, by the shares and its momentum, its suspension is carried, its clock is the count of its self-creations, and matter is a measured event.)

**Five rules the law left open, settled on 2026-09-18 (Boss's defaults, adopted by the model owner's "do what you think"; the owner may strike any of them).** (i) The step of a thing: its momentum accumulates the pushes, and when the component on an axis reaches the thing's content the thing changes heading to that axis and the momentum drops by that content; the staircase of the DDA walk of ray-momentum-turn-v2 is retired. (ii) A prefilled shadow's home is its owner: the prefill sets its steps to its Link distance from the owner's Node (for a loop, from the ring Node its line crosses), so its return arrives where the owner was; if the owner is there it is absorbed and re-released, if the owner has left it follows the trace, and where no trace exists it parks as a zero-step shadow until a thing of its owner passes. (iii) A seeded thing has its birth at its seed Node: missed at a mark, it walks back there and, the inverse split of a lone ray being the ray itself, leaves again on its original heading (on its own steps: a thing has steps, the real counts and the shadow does not; model owner, 2026-09-18, point 6). (iv) A shadow absorbed at the mark that is its home makes no event and is counted on the counter's shadow line, never as a click. (v) The record-as-owner field program of the schema-2 engine (the outward octant field, the absorb, exchange and rotation couplings of records, the mirror, slit, dissolve, capture and self-exclusion of that form, the load delay of departures) is retired with point 22: apparatus are things with declared tables, and no world of nature declares the old form. What this settles, from the runs of 2026-09-18: a screen beside a ring at rest counts nothing (E9's counting was a shadow made real without anyone paying); every push is balanced by its return (E11's books close without a source line); a draw at a mark of efficiency 1 decides nothing (E12); a shadow's bending is the mean field's (A6), and the delay table of A6's first form is not needed. Consequences the runs must show, not assume: whether a returning shadow comes home whole through the inverse splits of the spread (E11 (a) repeated), and what a mark reads of a thing that passes. What the engine implements as its only behaviour, with no declarable alternative (model owner, 2026-09-18): the bit set at birth, the push as a return, the mark absorbing things and returning shadows, the release from things alone, and the return that follows the trace; the elaboration of the trace register, the per-family absorb-or-pass at a mark and the naming of "thing" and "shadow" are the orchestrator's, flagged for the owner to strike. Amended the same day with point 3: a prefilled shadow has no steps and follows no trace; it is a field, absorbed wherever it reaches its owner and its owner's field elsewhere; the return the engine implements is that field, and the trace register is retired (point 22). Under the law of the shadow (2026-09-18, the evening, below): (i) reads for held content, whose step is a release toward the neighbour and an absorption there (reading (v) there); (ii) and (iv) have no object, there being no prefill, no home and no return; (iii) has none, since nothing is seeded as a thing and nothing walks back; (v) stands, the apparatus being held content with tables; what the engine implements as its only behaviour is feature 20's, beside the old. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says: no thing or shadow, no register, remainder or parked share, no return; an event's exit is one of seven, by the shares and its momentum, its suspension is carried, its clock is the count of its self-creations, and matter is a measured event.)
