**A thing does not emit; its shadows are given with the board and circulate (model owner, 2026-09-18, the same decision).** The field is not a stream: a thing does not release shadows interval after interval. Its shadows exist with the board, as a declared initial condition of the world (a declared profile, or the steady state of the declared things computed by the split table's mean field, booked as initial content and not as a source), their total the declared size of the thing's shadow set; a shadow that comes home, after a push, a mark or a mirror, leaves again from where the thing now is, so the field of a moving thing follows it with the delay of the path and nothing is ever created; what escapes the board is the only loss, and that is the box's artefact, not the model's. The release rule of section 3.5 ("every interval, the content resident at a Node says in all six headings that it is there") is superseded by this: the saying is the standing set of shadows, not a repeated emission. The size of the shadow set (content times a declared ratio, or a declared amount) is the model's constant for the strength of a force, as the charge is for its sign, and it is the model owner's to choose (section 5.5). Two rules of the same decision (model owner, 2026-09-18): a prefilled field is rounded to whole quanta per Node and heading and the fractional parts go into the Node's remainder registers of section 3.17, so nothing of the computed field is lost and the books open exact; and when a mark absorbs a thing, the mark becomes the home of that thing's shadows: each one that comes back is absorbed into the same counter, so every message returns to where the thing ended and the counter holds all that the thing was. The prefill's computation by the mean field and the re-release heading (back out along the line the shadow came home on, the table spreading it at the next Node) are the orchestrator's elaborations, flagged. The same day, points 22 and 24: the fractional parts are shadows parked at the Node, not registers; the mean field and the re-release spread are the Node's mixing, not the split table. Under the law of the shadow (2026-09-18, the evening, below) the emission of "A thing emits" stands and the emitter is held content, every interval in proportion to its content; nothing is given with the board and nothing comes home. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says: no thing or shadow, no register, remainder or parked share, no return; an event's exit is one of seven, by the shares and its momentum, its suspension is carried, its clock is the count of its self-creations, and matter is a measured event.)

**A thing emits; nothing is given with the board (model owner, 2026-09-18, the evening, superseding "A thing does not emit" of the same day: "it is surely not right that the shadows come with the board"; "a real emits shadow forever, a shadow disappears").** What forced it, measured the same day: under the count reading (point 16) a static force 1/r² is a steady stream, the same amount crossing every shell every interval, and a finite amount given once cannot hold it on any board: on the open board the field escaped (the A6 lane), on the closed board it never settled (the E11 lane: no fixed point and no cycle in 120 ticks, the content per Node swinging a hundredfold as the train circles the board and reconverges at the thing, the push on a probe rising with r, its sign changing every two or three intervals, and only the amplitude at the probe falling as 1/r). The decision: a thing releases shadows every interval, in proportion to its content (point 18's ratio is now a rate), and a shadow does not last: it disappears after its time. The field is the balance of the two, a stream, and its fixed point is the static field: 1/r in amplitude and 1/r² in flux out to the reach the lifetime gives, on an open or a closed board alike. The prefill remains only as a shortcut, the fixed point written at tick 0 instead of waited for. What follows: a change at the source propagates at the field's speed (causality kept, since the field stays on the board); a return that never reaches home fades instead of circling, so the third law is the symmetry of the two fields with a small, passing remainder; a thing born mid-run builds its field within a lifetime and the field of a thing that ended fades, so no field is orphaned; the books of the shadows carry an emission line and a sink line, equal at the fixed point, and the things' line stays exact with no source. Settled by the model owner the same evening: a shadow is never made to disappear ("we must not make a shadow disappear": physics gives no decay of a field in time, the photon's mass is bounded at 10⁻¹⁸ eV), so there is no lifetime and no new constant; the stream leaves the world at the edge of an open board ("an open board, then"), which stands for infinity as it does in physics: inside the board the fixed point is 1/r in amplitude and 1/r² in flux exactly, reached after about L√3 intervals, what leaves is booked on the shadows' line as escaped, and the amount on the board is bounded by the emission times the transit. This supersedes "The board of a run is closed" and "only closed worlds are tested" of the same day: the escape of the field that made the board closed was the stream doing what a stream does, with nothing yet feeding it; the confrontation runs are made on open boards. The orchestrator's elaborations, flagged: the emission rate per interval in whole quanta with a remainder, and the fixed point in integers. DERIVATIONS.md round 7 derives the fixed point, the transient, λ against Newton, Coulomb and the four tests of A6, before the engine implements it (feature 19). Point 7, the paragraph above, the closed-board paragraph and the settled rule (ii) read with this. The law of the shadow (the same evening, below) carries this as its reading (i): the emitter is held content, matter, and every ray it releases is a shadow; the rate is ρ in the second glossary, the orchestrator's name. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says: no thing or shadow, no register, remainder or parked share, no return; an event's exit is one of seven, by the shares and its momentum, its suspension is carried, its clock is the count of its self-creations, and matter is a measured event.)

**Everything begins and is realized at a marked Node (model owner, 2026-09-17).** A source is a Detector (section 3.19), so every ray's history begins at a marked Node; between marked Nodes it is content on paths, combining by phase where paths meet; a marked Node that draws 1 realizes one path of events, and its return cancels the others through their event. The picture of the world is the list of PASS clicks in the frame of the observer (sections 3.29 and 3.30), and nothing else is ever seen: the rendering of the board is the record's view, which no observer inside the world has, and the same run drawn as clicks only is the physical picture. Superseded on 2026-09-18 (the evening) by the law of the shadow (below): no mark draws and no return cancels a path, since nothing walks one; "events are created only at a detector" and "what the eye sees is events", the model owner's words, so the picture of the world is the list of events, each a whole quantum absorbed at held content. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says: no thing or shadow, no register, remainder or parked share, no return; an event's exit is one of seven, by the shares and its momentum, its suspension is carried, its clock is the count of its self-creations, and matter is a measured event.)

**The price.** No registry answers at a distance; pair identity is the trajectory. The accepted price: two Detectors at equal distance from the birth draw independently and the CHSH value for spacelike settings is at most 2; the joint law's value appears only when the second ray's path exceeds the round trip through the first Detector. Under the law of the shadow (2026-09-18, the evening, below) the price stands in its own form, reading (vii): Bell is two events of one field at two places, each born of the local field and the local setting, at most 2; the round trip through the first Detector has no object, since nothing returns, and the joint law's value without it is to be restated by round 8. (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: read as section 5.4's law of events says: no thing or shadow, no register, remainder or parked share, no return; an event's exit is one of seven, by the shares and its momentum, its suspension is carried, its clock is the count of its self-creations, and matter is a measured event.)

**The law of the shadow: only shadows and events (model owner, 2026-09-18, the evening; supersedes the bit of the law of the bit).** The model owner's words: "No real and shadow. There is only shadow. There are events, which are a whole quantum. That is all. The shadow spreads like a ray from the event." And the same evening, the steps to it: "what the eye sees is events"; "Bell measures an event"; "events are created only at a detector"; "an event is created when measuring", and measuring is a whole quantum changing state at a Node. The law: every ray on the board is a shadow, a quantum of a family in flight, moving one Link per interval and spreading by the Node's mixing (point 24) with its phase rotating by its content over K (light's content its amount); an event is a whole quantum at a Node that holds content, where it is absorbed, held or released again by the family's table; from the event the shadows spread. Nothing walks a path of its own. The orchestrator's reading, flagged for the owner, to be derived (DERIVATIONS.md round 8) and implemented as a new engine beside the old (feature 20, field-only-v1), the old one kept until the new one has repeated every confrontation: (i) matter is content held at Nodes (what the external body and the mark's counter already are), and it is the only place events happen: it releases shadows every interval in proportion to its content ("A thing emits"), absorbs the whole quanta that reach it by its family's table, and releases again; a click is an absorption; (ii) the push is an absorption: the momentum a whole quantum carries (with the family's sign, gravity's toward the source) times the holder's cross-section, its content for gravity and its charge for electricity (point 16 as amended), so the equivalence and Coulomb stay; the third law is the symmetry of the two fields, what escapes to the open edge carrying equal and opposite momenta; there is no return and no home; (iii) the wait reads the size (point 23 as amended) and is paid by every quantum, in flight or held: a held content's release slows in a potential (the clock, the redshift), and a quantum of light in flight steps slower where the gravity's size is larger (an index, n = 1 + 2GM/r: the bending of light and the Shapiro delay whole, not half); (iv) a quantum absorbed and released again carries the holder's phase and number: coherence is the last emitter, no inheritance, no orphan; (v) a held content moves by its momentum as point 21 says, the step a release and an absorption at the neighbour, so nothing outruns the field; light and matter share one c, the field's; (vi) the remainder rule (point 22) makes a whole quantum where the parked parts reach one, so a weak field gives one absorption at one Node, and the distribution over many is the amount's, Born by counting; (vii) Bell is unchanged: two events of one field at two places, each born of the local field and the local setting, at most 2. What round 8 must show before the new engine is trusted: that a held content is stable (its gravity and electricity free, its light paid), that its step keeps it at or below the field's speed, that a content in uniform motion absorbs no net momentum from its own field (inertia), that bound contents exchanging quanta give the ladder of hypothesis 12, and what a split and a pair creation are as tables. Points 1, 2, 9, 10, 13, 14 and 21 of the law of the bit read with this; features 16h and 19 fold into feature 20. Status (model owner, 2026-09-19, "put everything in main, and say the quantum feature must be tested; it must not be usable without the decisions"): the engine of the law of the shadow (feature 20, field-only-v1, merged as PR #327 beside the old engine) is **under test**, not the working law. It is repeated against every confrontation (E11, A5s, A6, A1, E9 on open boards) before the old engine is retired, and a world of its law is refused unless it declares the three decisions the round and the feature left to the owner: the number a transmitted quantum carries (a slit, a mirror or a plate keeping the number of what it re-emits, or its own), the wait's unit w (`wait_per_quantum`, round 8's w_GR = 1.316 √(G q_F) or another), and the gravity reading's multiplier ε_g (1, or the declared hierarchy). The old engine and the law of the bit stay usable as they are. (2026-09-19, later in the day, the model owner, given in Hebrew and translated: "We do not need these three things at all." There are no pending decisions and no gate: feature 20b, `shadow-gate-v1`, is not written, and the three keys are not added. The orchestrator's reading, flagged: the engine as it stands is the law's form, with the world's declared widths (ρ, K, N, and `wait_per_quantum`, 1 by default and 0 in the slit worlds, which the owner ruled stays as a declared width like the others, the same day: "there is only a delayed clock", and the key says only how much, one whole unit of size read costing n/d intervals); a quantum released again carries the last emitter's number (reading (iv)); the wait is the size read, one interval per whole unit, the stay at a Node of "One speed, and what is seen" below; and the gravity reading has no multiplier, G = ρ/(4π), so the hierarchy of section 55 (x) of DERIVATIONS.md stays an open question of the model, hypothesis 16's kind, not a parameter of a world. The engine stays under test until it has repeated the confrontations on open boards.) (2026-09-19, the evening, the model owner, given in Hebrew and translated: "The field is, in fact, a field of events. No confrontations are needed. Only tests that everything is as designed." One engine: the old engine of the law of the bit is retired the same day without the confrontation runs, and the engine of this law is the engine, its evidence the isolated tests that pin what the law says on minimal boards (`tests/test_field_only.py`) and the readings of its first worlds; the confrontations on open boards (E11, A5s, A6, A1, E9) are research runs to make when wanted and register in EXPERIMENTS.md, not a condition. The orchestrator's reading, flagged: "a field of events" is the law read from the Node: what the field is made of is the events at held content, an absorption or a release of a whole quantum, and the amount in transit between them; the deletion is recorded in MIGRATION.md.) (2026-09-19, the same evening, the model owner, given in Hebrew and translated: "Simplify everything, yes. What can be put as a parameter, put." The orchestrator's reading, flagged: a choice the law leaves open is a declared width of the world with today's behaviour as its default, never a constant in the engine; the first, the open question of what a matter shadow rotates by (round 8 section 52 (iv)), is `turns_in_flight` per family, whether its quanta turn their phase in flight by their amount over K, true for a paid family and false for a free one by default. The precisions of the arithmetic (the 32nds of an amplitude, the 256 of the cosine table, the ninths of the mixing) are not choices and stay.) (2026-09-19, later, the model owner, given in Hebrew and translated: "Put it in, without an experiment": the quantum of a family, `quantum`, the second declared width: the units that make one event at a holder that absorbs them, per number, the rest waiting in the holder's register until a whole is there; in the owner's words, a Detector counts whole photons, not halves of one, and where the next dot appears is the Node whose register fills first, Born by counting. Round 8's q_γ (S5, S6) as a width, 1 by default. The orchestrator's reading, flagged: the push of the units enters the holder as they are taken and the event is the click.) (2026-09-19, later, the model owner, given in Hebrew and translated, asked "constant frequency for light?" and, told that the engine's turn by the cell's amount slows as the field thins and stops a few Links from a lamp while a turn by the message keeps the frequency light was born with: "Yes, make it the default for light. That is, it is per family." So light turns by its message: `phase_turn` per family, `"quantum"` by default for a paid family, the family's `quantum` over K per Link walked, the same for every quantum of the family wherever it is and however diluted, a remainder carried per family, round 8's S5; `"none"` by default for a free family, a matter shadow's phase not turning in flight (section 52 (iv)); `"amount"`, the old rule, kept as a choice. The key `turns_in_flight` of the same morning is replaced by this one. The orchestrator's reading, flagged: the frequency of a family's field is set at birth and changes only by the stays at Nodes, the delayed clock; a quantum that waits does not turn, since it walks no Link; and with the default quantum of 1 the turn is 1/K per interval, so a world that wants light with a frequency declares its quantum, the photon.) (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: "shadow" and "held content" are two states of one thing, the event, in transit and measured; a click is a measurement, an event fixed at a place; reading (vi), the remainder rule, is superseded, nothing waits below one; the status line's `wait_per_quantum` says how much suspension a Port's exit derives from what is present; the rest reads as the law of events says.)

**Consistency table: the law of the bit against the law of the shadow (the orchestrator's summary, 2026-09-18, the evening, flagged).** One line per family of statements; the readings are those of the paragraph above. (2026-09-19: the fourth column, "The law of events says", added the same day for the paragraph after "One speed, and what is seen"; the first two columns are history, "There is no bit anymore".)

| Statements | The law of the bit said | The law of the shadow says | The law of events says (2026-09-19) | Where recorded |

| Measurement, Bell | The number of a pair travels through the birth event on a return; CHSH at most 2 for spacelike settings ("The price") | Two events of one field at two places, each born of the local field and the local setting, at most 2; unchanged (reading (vii)) | Two clicks at two detectors, each decided at its own Node by the arriving record and the local setting, the phase window; at most 2; the run designed by the physicist and the mathematician and made by an executing agent on a small board (made 2026-09-19: S = 2 exactly, the model's limit) | 5.4, "The price", "A pair"; 3.2, 3.20 annotated |

**One speed, and what is seen (model owner, 2026-09-19; a reading of the law of the shadow, not a change to it).** The model owner's words, given in Hebrew and translated: "A quantum passes, like everything, at the speed of light between Nodes." And the same day, on what is seen: what we see are events, and only through a Detector; at an event one whole quantum is fixed at that Node, completing the information there, and the rest spreads, the information about the event, at the speed of light. The owner also asked to read the picture, for the moment, without the word "shadow": there is held amount at a Node, amount in transit on the Links, and the event that moves amount from the one to the other; "shadow" is only the name of the amount in transit, and light and matter are two families that differ by their table. The orchestrator's readings, flagged: (a) every transfer between two Nodes is one Link in one interval, for everything, and there is no other speed on a Link; all slowness is a stay at a Node. (b) A held content's speed is the intervals it stays between one step and the next, the step itself a release that crosses the Link at the field's speed and an absorption at the neighbour (reading (v)), so nothing outruns the field. (c) Light's index in a potential, n = 1 + 2GM/r (reading (iii)), is the wait charged at the Node, intervals owed for the size read there, after which the quantum moves one Link per interval again. (d) A content's clock advances while it stays, so the redshift is the stays it accumulated: speed and clock are two readings of one counter. (e) The absorbed quantum is the record of the event, the amount that joined the content, the push that entered its momentum and the phase, completed at the local holder alone; what the rest of the board receives is the release from the event, carrying the last emitter's phase and number (reading (iv)) and no message, so locality holds without a report. Nothing here changes a rule: point 21 (every ray moves one Link per interval) and readings (iii) and (v) stand, and the engine already works this way, the wait a hold per Node and the transfer one Link per interval ([Highlights coverage](HIGHLIGHTS_IMPLEMENTATION.md)). Whether the word "shadow" stays as the name of the amount in transit, in the documents and the code, is not decided here. (Decided 2026-09-19, the law of events: it does not stay; "There is no shadow and no real; on the board there are only events.") (Under the law of events, 2026-09-19: a stay at a Node is the suspension of a Port's exit, derived from the events present and not a hold with a count; "the engine already works this way" is said of the engine of the law of the shadow, which parks and counts what the law of events removes, see Highlights coverage.)

**The law of events: no fields, no matter, no registers; only events (model owner, 2026-09-19, after the one engine and `phase_turn`).** The model owner's words, given in Hebrew and translated, in the order they were said. On the register of the mixing: "What is that register there? We said there is no split. No split." "Let the assumption be that there are no registers. Everything is derived from vector operations and the like." "I want to move to the assumption that the Node handles an event: by the assumption, it simply passes the event on to the next Nodes, by the clock's tick and by the Port. There is no memory of something that stays there and has to be handled afterwards; there is no such thing. A single quantum simply passes in one direction, and that is the idea that helps us very much: because of it, it no longer spreads. And once it no longer spreads, it can be returned to the source. This is very, very critical. We have no remainders, but we also lose no information, because the quantum goes in the right direction. It may be by direction, it may change its direction or something like that, but a quantum loses no information. And on the other hand it does not spread either." On how, with no law for it: "There is no law; you do not know that it is a quantum. You try to do what it has to do for every side, and it sees that it simply has to set one side, because it has one, and it has to keep all the conservations the cell has. The wait is by exit and Port; the exit is general to the Node, not tied to the fields there. What is obtained from this generically, and I want it obtained generically, is that a single quantum does not split. I am speaking in the space of events: in the space of events the quantum leaves in one direction, and not like something that splits, which is not a single quantum." On the wait: "When a Port is computed, it looks at the fields that act there, at the mass or the charge or whatever, and decides, completely generically, how much suspension it needs because of the clock." On fields: "And when I say fields, they are not really fields. What enters there is events, not quanta, because we have no field. We are an event-time space; the events that sit there act physically on the current event. There are no fields; a field is an event. When an event enters from a Port, it is handled completely generically." On matter: "There is no matter either. Matter, as it is defined, is a measured event. That is matter. And a measured event: you measure. You can measure a single quantum at a specific place. That is the idea." And: "Record it in Highlights in my words, and make sure it contradicts nothing else." And on the name, the same day, asked whether the word "shadow" leaves the code: "There is no shadow and no real; on the board there are only events." So the word leaves the model with the law: the engine of the law of events calls what is on the board an event, in transit or measured, and a world file selects that law by that name; the engine of the law of the shadow keeps its names until it is replaced. The definitions, as the orchestrator read them back to the owner, who went on to the next each time: (1) one thing, the event, with a place (a Node), a time (an interval) and a record (amount, phase, number, momentum, heading); "field", "shadow", "quantum" and "matter" are names of events in states. (2) An event in transit leaves a Node through one Port, whole, one Link per interval, with all its information; a single event leaves in one direction; many leave in up to six, each whole. (3) A measured event is an event fixed at a place, a single quantum measured at one Node in one interval; what was matter and what was a click are the same; a measured event stays at its Node and acts on the events that enter there after it, its record being what was "mass" or "charge". (4) A Node in an interval is the events that entered through its Ports and the events measured there, and one generic computation: the records read by the tables, without names; the sides' shares from the vectors (the coherent sum, the squared sizes, the momenta); whole units placed; the exit per Port with its suspension derived from the reading; a measurement where the table says. Nothing of the computation is kept: no remainder, no parked share, no count. (5) What is seen is measured events only. (6) Declared: the families' tables (what a measured event does with an entering one) and the first measured events of a world; the rest is derived.

The orchestrator's readings, flagged, each for the owner to strike: (a) the Node is a function of the interval, of what is present, with no state of its own; (b) with fewer units than a side's whole share, the whole goes to one side by the conservations of the cell, the heading of its momentum when it has one, so a lone event is a beam and the wave is the crowd, and "a lone shadow mostly turns back" (point 24) no longer holds while the shares of point 24 stand as the computation; (c) the suspension is not a count the Node keeps: it is decided again in every interval from the events present, and what makes the decision change from one interval to the next is the phase the present events carry, the clock of their sources, "because of the clock"; whether a count rides on the event instead, the owner has not said; (d) no split below a whole leaves the register of `quantum` (the units of one number waiting at a holder) empty by construction, so `quantum` is the size of a family's unit in the books and not a threshold, and the phase turn in flight, with no remainder per family, is a whole number of steps of the circle per Link, declared per family; (e) the return to the source: an event that never spread is on one line, so with its heading reversed it retraces to its source without a trace, the third law per event. Reading (c) answered the same day, the owner asked whether the suspension is decided again each interval or carried: "The event carries it; note that the next event is delayed." The orchestrator's reading, flagged: the count of the suspension rides in the event's record, set at the Port's exit from what is present and counted down by the event itself, so the Node counts nothing; and an exit keeps its order: an event that reaches an exit where one is still suspended waits behind it, its count no less than the one ahead's plus one, so a suspension delays what follows and nothing overtakes on a lane; the Node keeps no queue, the events present are the queue. Readings (b) and (e) answered the same day: asked whether every event carries momentum in its record from birth, without exception, so that a single event always goes by its momentum: "Yes, every event carries momentum from birth. By the momentum." And, after the fixing mechanism of the principles below was compared with the law: "So we do not want a return to the source." So (b) reads without "when it has one", every event having a momentum, and (e) is struck: nothing returns, neither as a report nor as the third law's return; the third law is paid at emission, the recoil the emitter takes, and at measurement, the push that enters the measured event. Corrected the same day, the orchestrator, flagged: that holds for a paid family, whose event carries the emitter's momentum and gives it back at measurement (light's pressure); a free family's release costs nothing and takes no recoil, its reader applies the family's sign (gravity toward the source), and the third law is the symmetry of the two fields with what escapes, as the law of the shadow says (reading (ii)), since attraction and conservation per event cannot both hold for a field that pushes by absorption. The clock, the same day, asked whether a measured event's record holds its clock, the intervals since it was measured, so that its rates are read off it: "It returns to itself, in fact, at a generic level, understand?" and "Every clock tick there is self-creation too, that is, no transfer to the next Port. Does this explain it generically?" The orchestrator's reading, flagged: it does. At every interval every event is created at its next place from its record, and the next place is one of seven, the six neighbours or here; a measured event is one whose exit is here without end, a suspended event one whose exit is here for its count, an event in transit one created at a neighbour, and the released shares are what goes to the sides; one operation for all, no second kind of thing. The clock is the count of creations in the record, advanced at each; a measured event's release and its phase are read off it by whole division, so nothing accumulates and a small content releases seldom but releases; and an event in transit does not turn at all: a transfer is not a tick of its clock ("every clock tick there is self-creation, that is, no transfer to the next Port"), so its phase is the one it was born with, light's frequency is the emitter's clock stamped on the stream and constant in flight, as section 3.3 said from the start ("nothing is accumulated on the way"), and a merged bundle needs no count, a suspended one keeping the larger of the merged. A suspended event's clock does not tick during its count, so a measured event that reads a large size releases and turns slower, the redshift, and matter that steps often ticks less, the dilation of motion. So the phase turn needs no key: the per-Link turn of feature 23 (`phase_turn`, the same morning) is superseded and its key goes with the engine (confirmed the same day: "Correct, an event does not turn."); `quantum` is the unit's content for a paid family. Details for the engine's contract, the orchestrator's, not laws: the seven slots per Node and number, six lanes and here, are the whole state; the here input's shares to the sides come from the table's rate and the Ports' inputs' shares from the vectors, one computation; the suspension's width is a whole number of intervals per unit of size read; a tie of the momentum between headings is broken toward the current heading, then the lower Port; a measured event's step is its creation at the neighbour's here slot, into an occupied Node refused as today; the books are one ledger in content units, a paid family's units at their exchange rate; a merge of one number at a Node is a coherent sum, one record per exit, the individual phases in the sum, which is the interference itself and no loss to the books. Consequences to know, not contradictions: a single quantum takes one path and does not interfere with itself, interference being of many of one number; a part a crowded Node turns back is apportioned again at the next Node while it is three units or more, and goes by its momentum once it is one or two, so a lamp's near field mixes and its far field is rays (corrected the same day from "returns to its momentum's line": the shares are apportioned by the largest remainder, the ties in the interval's Port order, and the momentum decides only a group with no whole for any side).

The owner, the same day, on a detector's sensitivity: "If a detector sits on several Nodes, it can know, as it were, that there was some kind of event between them, no?" The orchestrator's reading, flagged: yes, and only so: a detector is measured events on several Nodes, each recording only what entered it (the amount, the number, the phase, the momentum, its clock at the measurement), and what one Node cannot know the records together can: one number at two Nodes is one emission that reached both, so there was an event; the two clocks and one Link per interval bound where and when it was; the momenta say from where; the phases over many Nodes are the pattern of a crowd. A detector's sensitivity is its Nodes and its record's fields, and nothing else: what reached no Node of it is unknowable (principle 7), the reading of the records together is the audit's or, inside the world, events between the detector's Nodes at one Link per interval, and the only agreement two Nodes can have about "the same event" is the number, given at birth (Bell at most 2). The run's record already lists every measurement with its interval, Node, number, amount, push and phase, so this reading needs no mechanism. The owner, going on: "a kind of detector sensitivity"; "and then a detector, as it were, knows how to detect beyond a single quantum, because it says: in this region..." The orchestrator's reading, flagged: a Node measures one quantum at one place; a detector over a region measures many and says "an event in this region": its statement is about the emission, the event that released what it caught, one by the number, placed by the clocks and the momenta within a region as small as its Nodes and its catch allow. What is seen is never a quantum but an event inferred from many quanta over a region, and a detector's sensitivity is the size of that region and the count it needs; a threshold, the smallest bundle a table measures, below which an arrival passes, is a declared width of the table if wanted, 1 by default. The owner, closing it: "So one can, in fact, see a quantum in a detector sensitive to a Node. And every detector must state what its sensitivity is, in fact, and that is exactly the uncertainty principle." The orchestrator's reading, flagged: a detector of one Node sees a quantum at a place and learns of its emission only a line (the momentum) and a time; a detector over a region sees the pattern of a crowd, the phases and momenta of many, which is the wavelength, and not the place of each; the declared sensitivity, the region and the count it needs, fixes which of the two a measurement can say, and that is the model's uncertainty: a width of the measurement, declared, not of the quantum, whose record holds its place and its momentum exactly and which the audit display can read whole while the detector cannot (principle 7). Nothing random is in it. For the engine: a detector in a world file is a named set of measured events with one table, its sensitivity its Nodes and, if declared, its threshold; the run's record reports its measurements per detector as well as per Node, so that its statement, "an event in this region", is read off the record. The owner's summary of it: "This in fact merges what was once field and event into one thing in the system." And the owner's closing words of the day, the charge for the document and the engine: "Go for everything. Make sure that the Highlights file, at once in git, explains everything without contradictions. There is no shadow, no real. There are only events on the event board. There are detectors by sensitivity. That is it. Everything must be generic in the engine, without registers. There are no draws. There are only opening events that spread by the physics of the engine." The orchestrator's reading, flagged: the opening events are the world file's declared measured events and events in transit at the start, and the engine's physics is the one computation of section 5.4; nothing is injected afterwards and nothing is drawn. Later the same day, the owner: "Just make sure that every kind of external apparatus works with the sensitivity, as receivers or as emitters" (the threshold of a detector gates every response of its Nodes, `read`, `measure` and `rerelease` alike, a smaller bundle passing; a release reads no threshold; `test_detector_sensitivity`); "There is no bit anymore" (the bit of the law of the bit is gone from the model as from the engine; the column of the consistency table below is history); and, asked whether a Bell run can be made, "Can we do a Bell experiment with an emitter and two detectors, Alice and Bob? An executing agent does it after the mathematician and the physicist approved. A small board." Told that the engine lacks a measurement setting, the owner approved it as a declared width: "Approve the phase window as a declared width of a detector, and of the emitter too, no?" The orchestrator's reading, flagged: one generic key, `phase_window`, a setting on the circle of N steps and the half circle around it; on a detector's table entry per family, a click only when the arriving phase minus the setting falls in the window, otherwise the bundle passes, decided at the Node from the arriving record and the local setting, no draw and no register; on a lamp, a release only at the self-creations whose clock phase falls in its window, the release stamped with that phase, so that a lamp with a window is a source of a declared phase and one without cycles through the circle with its clock. The Bell run (A2) is designed by the physicist and the mathematician on this width and made by an executing agent on a small board, registered in EXPERIMENTS.md; the width itself is a feature with its isolated test before the run. The same day, the width implemented (`test_phase_window`, [Highlights coverage](HIGHLIGHTS_IMPLEMENTATION.md)) with the contract the two reviewers pinned, the orchestrator's, flagged: the window is the centred half circle, the arriving phase minus the setting within a quarter circle either way, exactly N / 2 steps; the phase read is that of the bundle of one number at the Node, the nearest step of its coherent sum; the threshold gates first, the window second, and a bundle outside the window passes with a `pass` record carrying the phase read and the setting, so that every measurement and every pass is read off the record (the two sentences above and in ENGINE.md that said the record carried the phase became true with this feature); a lamp with a window releases only at the self-creations whose clock phase is in it, and its clock advances and its phase turns at every self-creation whether or not it released; the phase tables read a single arrival's step back exactly up to N = 64 and by one step off beyond it, so a run that reads phases declares N = 64. The reviewers' verdict on the run, recorded before it is made: with a deterministic local window each click is a function of the arriving phase and the local setting alone, so CHSH is at most 2 as an identity on the record, the correlation is the triangle 1 - 4k / N, and the CHSH settings give S = 2 exactly, the model's limit (A2, outcome 1 against the 2015 data) and not a fluctuation; the owner, told so: "Yes to both", the run to be made and registered as that limit.

The owner also gave the merge owner a standing authorization for the day, to merge every pull request whose CI is green, and "fix bugs if they come up". The run made the same day (the executing agent, commit 0b2027bd, source fingerprint 53a70962c5f2c8c860a0895dbc9d6cb7cf125d53aa3d5f0ce6aa5464c79a87f9; [EXPERIMENTS.md](EXPERIMENTS.md), "A2, under the law of events", and [examples/events/bell](../examples/events/bell/README.md)): ten worlds on a board of 21 x 1 x 1 Nodes, N = 64, a lamp with a window between Alice's two windowed Nodes and Bob's, 128 pairs per run; E(0, 8) = +1/2, E(0, 24) = -1/2, E(16, 8) = +1/2, E(16, 24) = +1/2, so S = 2 exactly, and on the quadruple (0, 8), (0, 12), (4, 8), (4, 12) S' = 3/2 exactly where the cosine would give 1.739; every E is the triangle 1 - 4k / N, the controls are +1, -1 and 0, no-signalling is exact (a side's click ages do not depend on the other side's setting), every click and every pass is read off the record, and the books balance at every interval; registered as the model's limit, A2 outcome 1 against the 2015 data, and not a law of nature. The orchestrator's reading, flagged: the two words named one operation with two exits, created at a neighbour (what was the field, the spreading) or created here (what was the event, the fixing); with the exit a Port among seven, nothing is left that is a field and not an event, and the unification of 2026-09-17 ("One ray, one catalog") and of 2026-09-18 ("only shadows and events") ends in one word and one operation. What this changes in the engine of the law of the shadow, which parks ninths, counts the wait, carries remainders and assembles quanta in a register, is listed in [Highlights coverage](HIGHLIGHTS_IMPLEMENTATION.md); implemented the same evening as `events-v1`, the one engine, shown by tests of the code and not by runs ("without experiments"), the coverage naming each test. Nothing is deleted from this document; the paragraphs this law changes carry a dated sentence. Later the same day, after the Bell run, the model owner, given in Hebrew and translated: "An important point for the Highlights document, at once: the uncertainty is in the detector and not in the board of events. Another point: the forces known in physics are what the detector measures. And therefore the coupling is not one-to-one. What the detector measures is not what is on the board. The board is of events. And that is what is important to do well." The orchestrator's reading, flagged: (i) the board is exact: every event's record holds its amount, phase, number, momentum, heading and counts as whole integers, the Node's one computation is fixed by what is present, and nothing on the board is uncertain or drawn; the uncertainty is the width a detector declares, its Nodes (the region), its threshold (the count it needs), its phase window (the phases it answers) and its clock, and two detectors of different widths read different things of the same events while neither reads a record whole, which only the audit display does; so an uncertainty relation is a relation between declared widths of a measurement and never a law of the board (the sentence above, "that is exactly the uncertainty principle", stands, and is placed: in the detector). (ii)

On the board there are events with momentum, in transit or measured, and one operation: an arriving event's momentum enters the measured event whose table measures it, and a suspended event counts. A force known in physics is what a detector, a measured event with its table, its family, its charge, its region and its threshold, measures of the events that reach it; the same events read by another detector are another force or none, and one force as known is the reading of more than one operation of the board (what a detector calls gravity is the push of a free family's events, toward the emitter, and also the suspension that slows a clock where the size is large; what it calls light's pressure is the same push by a paid family's events). So the coupling of a force's name to an operation of the board is not one-to-one, and the names in this document and in the table below, gravity, electricity, light's pressure, redshift, dilation, are names of what a detector measures, never operations of the board; the board has its one operation and the family's kind, charge and tables in a world file declare what its measured events are sensitive to. (iii) The board of events is what must be made well: the engine's tests are of the board alone; a force is read off the record through a detector's declared width, as the Bell run read its correlations off clicks and passes; no test and no run compares an operation of the board to a force by its name, and the engine recognizes nothing by a physical name, as before. Nothing changes in the engine today; the correspondences with forces, here and in [ENGINE.md](ENGINE.md), are readings of the record by a detector, to be stated as such where they are made. The same day, issue #338 (a probe on `main`: two runs that differ only in the phase of a unit measured by `measure` leave the board identical after the click and differ only in the click's record, while under `pass` the board differs) was read against the law and closed as the law's own statement, the orchestrator's reading, flagged, confirmed by the owner ("I confirm; close 338 with the sentences"): at a click the phase is read, as this paragraph says, and the reading is the detector's record; the phase of a measured event is its clock and takes nothing from what it measures, or its rates off the clock would depend on what fell on it; the amount joins and the momentum enters, and those two the board keeps; a click is the border between the board and the detector, one way ("we do not want a return to the source"), so the board after a click does not tell the phase measured and the run's record is the detector's measurement, not a history outside the model; "a quantum loses no information" is said of transit, where nothing splits or leaves a remainder, and not of measurement. Stated in [ENGINE.md](ENGINE.md), step 3; no engine change. The same day, the owner, on the suspension, given in Hebrew and translated: "But how did you determine how the mass affects the clock per Port?"; "Everything works per Port, no? There are seven, right? What is r?"; "But at the Node, in our model, it is per Port, without r?"; and, told that the count was the one reading at the Node and not at the Port: "I confirm; align the suspension per Port, with an agent." The orchestrator's reading, flagged: r is not on the board; it is a detector's reading, the count of Links between an emission and a click inferred from clocks and momenta, and "1 / r" and "1 / r^2" are readings of the record over shells, not quantities of the Node. At the Node everything is per Port and without r: the arrivals per Port, the leaving amplitude per Port, the wholes per side, the seven exits; the one exception was the count of the suspension, derived from the free families' size summed over the six Ports and written on the whole number at the Node, which the orchestrator had justified by the isotropy of the slowing, a detector's reading taken as a law, withdrawn. The rule, as the owner approved it: an event leaving by a Port carries the count derived at that exit, the free families' size of what leaves by the same Port in the same interval (the leaving amplitude of the mixing divided by three, in 32nds, whose squares over the six Ports sum to the square of the Node's size) times the declared width; the lane waits for its count and what is placed behind on the lane waits with it, the larger count kept; the seventh exit, here, the measured event's, reads what arrives at its Node, unchanged. Whether the slowing is the same in every direction is then measured by a detector and not assumed (for a lone arrival of a free family the exit back toward its source reads twice the others). Implemented by an agent as the engine's next feature with its isolated test ([Highlights coverage](HIGHLIGHTS_IMPLEMENTATION.md)). Withdrawn the same day, before any code changed (the agent was stopped as it started): shown that under the rule as proposed a unit heading toward the source waits twice as long as one heading away or across, the owner: "That is a serious bug, no?"; "Is this not in the JSON definitions? That is the standard, no?"; "What is correct and generic? And known from other simulators in physics?"; "Verify again"; and, verified, "Go for it" (the engine kept). The orchestrator's reading, flagged, with the numbers read off the engine at main: for a lone arrival of 16 units the Node's size is 128 on every exit while the exit rule would read 85 back toward the source and 42 elsewhere; in the field of a content of 2^16 at four Links the exit rule reads 225 toward the source, 199 away and 122 across, and on a diagonal Node 111 against 54; the free families' leaving shares are directed by the mixing (four ninths back for a lone arrival, the symmetric lossless six-Port scatter, reflection -2/3, the same as a TLM node), so any reading per exit is anisotropic and only the Node's size, the coherent sum of its arrivals per Port, is the same in every direction; that same count on every exit is the isotropic index n = 1 + 2GM / r c^2 that gives the bending and the Shapiro delay a detector reads, and the exit rule would give them in another ratio. What the other lattice schemes do (lattice gas and lattice Boltzmann: collide, then stream, a scalar property at the node; TLM: scatter, then connect, a slower medium a stub at the node, a seventh port whose pulse is scattered again the next step; FDTD: a permittivity per cell): the local property is a scalar of the node and the two steps are the node's computation and the transport, as here. So the engine at main stands and is the law: the size is read at the Node from the arrivals per Port, without r, the same for the six exits; the wait is the seventh exit, here, the event carrying its count, and after the count the Node computes it again with what joined it; the order on a lane is the order of the arrival slots per Port. What the world file declares is the width `suspension` (intervals per whole unit of size, 0 to switch it off), as the standard of declared widths says; where the size is read is the one computation and not a key, as a per-Link phase turn was not kept as a key once it was not the law. Stated in [ENGINE.md](ENGINE.md), step 2; no engine change. Later the same day, the physics-rule review of the coupling series (the readings pinned before its runs, section 6) found five things, recorded here for the owner: (1) a bug, fixed under the standing authorization ("fix bugs if they come up"): the engine read a measured event's suspension before its self-creation, so in a steady field of one whole unit or more it never self-created again, its clock frozen and not slowed (a probe at twelve Links from a content of 2^24 aged 15 in 60 intervals and stopped); now a measured event pays its count, self-creates, and then reads and owes the next count, one self-creation per k + 1 intervals for a size of k whole units, as this paragraph and the transit's rule say (`test_event_suspension` (b) re-pinned and (c), a steady field, added); (2) a limit of the model as declared: the suspension reads a size, an amplitude, which goes as the square root of the count, so the slowing of a clock at r from a content M goes as sqrt(M) / r while the potential of nature goes as M / r, and no single width `suspension` serves two contents (the width that gives 2GM / r c^2 for M = 2^24 is about 220 and grows as sqrt M); no local reading gives M / r, the count giving M / r^2 and the size sqrt(M) / r; (3) the third law with unequal contents: two contents 4 : 1 eight Links apart read pushes in the ratio 1.28 on 21^3, the symmetry of the two fields holding for identical streams only, since each sits on one Node of the other's axis where the momentum per unit varies along the axis; (4) no Newtonian regime on these boards: the flux at r is about 62 000 / r^2 units per interval, far above one unit of momentum, so every probe steps one Link per interval within two intervals of its first push and merges into the source at 2r + 1; G is unreadable from step rates, while the equivalence is an identity (contents 1, 4, 16 make the same steps); (5) a measured event's suspension reads the sizes of all other numbers of every family, light included, while an event in transit reads the free families only, two rules where the law has one, the owner's to decide. The same day, asked "how is the interference experiment with two different light sources going", a run was made: two lamps of one family and one clock twenty Links apart, no wall, a screen at 69 Links, read a profile equal to the sum of the two single lamps' within 1 %, no fringes, because the mixing sums coherently per number (the orchestrator's choice in node-mixing-v2, one number one group) and not over all that is present at the Node as this law's words say; nature's independent lasers do interfere (Magyar and Mandel 1963; Pfleegor and Mandel 1967, one photon at a time). Whether events of different numbers mix at a Node is the owner's to decide; if they do, the coherent sum runs over all arrivals, the sides' shares are common, and each number's units are placed by the common shares, the momentum per number exact as now.

The owner's words that followed, the same day, given in Hebrew and translated, recorded as asked ("Write in git Highlights everything I described here and see if it solves it for you"): on the flat boards: "Is it not always better to run first with one of the axes at 1 on an open board?" (the orchestrator: a bar for an isolated rule, yes; for a spreading, no, since on an open board every Node loses a share through the two exits of the short axis at every interval, two ninths for a lone arrival, and the crowd decays in a few Links; a cube for the far field, a slab of nine for beams and screens); "How do other simulators do it?" (a lattice of its own dimension, D2Q9 against D3Q19, the two-dimensional TLM node with four Ports; and declared boundaries on the faces: absorbing, reflecting, symmetry, periodic; a thin three-dimensional lattice with absorbing faces is a leaky guide for them too); "And if it is closed on the z axis?"; and the decision: "As a run parameter, yes, so it is approved; each experiment will decide what to run." The orchestrator's reading, flagged: an axis may be declared periodic in the world file (`boundary` per axis, open the default and the meaning of "the edge is infinity"; "closed" still refused); on a periodic axis what leaves one face is created at the other, nothing escapes there and the momentum stays on the board; with a size of 1 the two exits of that axis return to the same Node the next interval, a four-Port Node with a one-interval stub, as the two-dimensional TLM node with a stub; implemented as the engine's next feature with its isolated test. On the five findings: "For each, always what the consequences are for the game on the board; the game on the board means the event board over time", and "only what requires a decision": the three decisions left are the slowing as sqrt(M) / r, which families a measured event's suspension reads, and whether events of different numbers mix at a Node. Asked whether the first is the coupling of mass to the slowing of a clock: yes; its strength is the declared width, as every coupling is a detector's calibration, but its form, the square root, is the board's, what the exit reads, and no width turns sqrt(M) into M. And the principle, the owner's: "The transformation must always distinguish between the game board and nature, which is what is observed through detectors at a particular point." Checked against the first decision, the orchestrator, flagged: it does not dissolve it. What a detector observes at a point of the board is a count (M / r^2), a size (sqrt(M) / r), a phase, a momentum and a clock; every product of powers of the count and the size gives M^(x + y/2) r^(-2x - y), and M / r would need x + y / 2 = 1 with 2x + y = 1, which no x, y satisfy; so no reading at a point gives M / r, and the potential of nature is the count summed along a path, not a thing at a point. Nature's own readings at points decide: the redshift of the Sun against the Earth's surface is in the ratio of M / R, about 3000, where sqrt(M) / R gives about 5; and GPS clocks read 1 - R / r, the 1 / r, not the 1 / r^2 a count would give. So the slowing as sqrt(M) / r stands as a limit of the model to be registered, unless a local reading no one has named is found; the decision is the owner's.

The owner's decisions that followed the same day, given in Hebrew and translated: on the suspension, "Maybe the slowing of a clock depends on more couplings and not only on m? Light is an event like any other, no?", and on Einstein: "The question is how to couple a property to the computation; in Einstein's not only m curves space; by Einstein one must curve"; told that the universal coupling means every event present suspends, with one fractional width [n, d] so that a crowd does not freeze itself: "Accepted, but it is the same general solution, no?" (yes: the suspension reads the presence of everything at the Node, count times n over d; the amplitude is no longer what it reads); asked what a family declares: its kind, charge and quantum, and whether it has a phase circle; the frequency is the emitter's clock, not the family's; polarization is not in the record (a second circle if ever wanted); and Einstein's field has waves with phase and two polarizations (LIGO), which a diffusive field of matter would not carry, so gravitational waves would be a paid family of their own later. On the mixing per number: "Is this in the engine?" (yes, mixing.py, not a key) and "What is right for genericity?" (the sum over all present; the number is a label the detector reads). Then: "So think again and say what is needed for certain"; the orchestrator's five, flagged: (1) the coherent sum at a Node runs over all numbers present; (2) the suspension reads the presence of everything with one width [n, d], in transit as at a measured event; (3) since no reading at a point of a coherent field gives M / r, the field of matter is a family without phase, its arrivals scattering per Port on their own, so that its presence obeys Laplace and goes as M / r in space and as the logarithm on the plane, or else sqrt(M) / r is a registered limit; (4) with (3), the push of a free family reads the net flow of the arrivals, amount times heading, not the momentum stamped at birth, or the force would go as M / r; (5) on a periodic axis a measured event's step wraps as the transit does. The owner: "Everything now. Revert if the report says otherwise than Einstein. Confirm that this is right." Confirmed by the orchestrator with the guard that (3) and (4) merge only after the physics-rule reviewer's numbers on the diffusive field (presence times r constant in space, the logarithm on the plane, one width serving two contents), and are reverted if those numbers do not give Einstein's M / r and M / r^2; (1), (2) and (5) merge on green. The coupling series C, made the same day on the plane (121 x 121 x 1, z periodic; EXPERIMENTS.md), read before these changes: every identity held (equivalence 1 : 4 : 16 at every record, superposition exact, the retardation front as derived, the electric reading exact, the clock's replay exact); the plane's far field read count times r constant, flow times 2 pi r about 1, size times sqrt r constant, slopes -0.99, -1.05, -0.48; the third law with contents 4 : 1 read 1.26 and 1.21; the momentum stamped at birth read 1.45 times the flow (the back-scattered shares carry outward labels inward), the reading that (4) replaces; the escape reached 0.95 of the emission only after a thousand intervals.

The owner, the same day: "We have quantum gravity, right?"; "Quantum gravity is behind a detector, no? We need a detector for it, no?"; "There is a new detector, look"; "In git." The orchestrator's reading, flagged: in this model gravity is whole events on the one board with the one computation, its slowing of clocks whole counts, and a claim of quantum gravity is only what a declared detector reads at a point: a measured event with `measure` and `threshold` 1, one click per whole unit. Nature's new detector (Tobar, Manikandan, Beitel and Pikovski, Nature Communications 2024, under construction in 2026) reads single quantum jumps of a cooled massive resonator during a gravitational wave, one graviton absorbed with its energy; that graviton is a quantum of a wave emitted by accelerating masses, not of the static field, so its counterpart here is a paid family of gravitational waves, not yet declared, and not the free family whose units carry no energy. Registered as the planned entry A9 in [EXPERIMENTS.md](EXPERIMENTS.md). Asked whether this is well defined here, the orchestrator, flagged: the distinction is (a free family's release costs nothing and takes no recoil, the static field; a paid family's unit costs `quantum` and takes recoil, light and a wave alike; a detector of whole units is a measured event with `measure` at threshold 1), and two things are not, recorded as gaps: (1) what emits a gravitational wave: nature's is the acceleration of masses, and the model has no rule by which a measured event that is pushed releases a paid family at its own cost, so a wave family would be a lamp under another name; (2) the energy of a quantum: nature's graviton and photon carry h times the frequency, while a unit of a paid family here costs the constant `quantum` whatever the emitter's clock, so a blue lamp's click and a red lamp's carry the same content and the photoelectric effect, the first quantum reading, is not reproduced.

The owner, told so: "I approve the proposal; record the two gaps in Highlights; there are clear definitions for external apparatus; look at what git has as a detector." The proposal approved: a release costs the emitter by its phase rate: a unit of a paid family costs `quantum` times the emitter's phase steps per self-creation (its content over K, the whole part off its clock), carries that as its momentum along its heading and gives it to what measures it, so that the energy of a click is proportional to the emitter's frequency, E = h f with h the declared `quantum`; a family without a phase circle turns by nothing and costs nothing, which is the free family, so the two kinds become one rule. Implemented the same day (flagged, the orchestrator's record of what the code does): the turn s is `by_clock(age, content, K)` read from the content before that self-creation's releases; a lamp releases only when s > 0, at most held // (quantum x s) units, each costing quantum x s, carrying that content and the momentum quantum x s along its heading, the recoil taken; the content is carried per slot in transit beside amount, phase and momentum and goes with the units at every Node exactly as the momentum does; `measure` adds the carried content to the measuring event; the books carry a content line balanced at every interval. Two facts the implementation fixed that the approval did not say: a free family's units carry content 0 and cost nothing whatever their turn, but their momentum label stays quantum x amount along the heading, since that label is what the sides read for a lone unit (zeroing it sent every lone free unit back and changed the field); and `kind` still decides the push rule (free by the net flow, paid by the carried momentum), the free release off the world's `release`, and the parser's keys, while the cost, content and momentum of a paid unit follow s alone. The Bell worlds run unchanged (S = 2, 326 criteria); a lamp of a phase-less paid family never releases, which the parser does not yet refuse. What git has as a detector today: a `detectors` entry of the world, a name, the positions of its measured events (each in one detector, each on a measured event) and a `threshold` (`DetectorDefinition`); per table entry a `phase_window`; the run's record per detector (the amount measured and the clicks per family) and per click (the tick, the Node, the number, the amount, the push, the phase); a fixed measured event is the external body of 2026-09-17 under this law; the sensitivity of a detector is its Nodes, its threshold and its window (TERMINOLOGY.md, ENGINE.md). Implemented next as the engine's feature with its isolated test, after the field of matter. The physics-rule reviewer's numbers on the diffusive field arrived the same day, measured on a standalone integer model against the exact solution of the discrete Laplace equation (121 x 121 on the plane, z periodic; 31^3 in space): the presence of a phase-less crowd is the Laplace Green's function, log(R / r) on the plane with the slope 1.89 q per e-fold (the pin 6 / pi = 1.91 from D = 1 / 12, derived and measured exactly) and (3 / pi)(1 / r - 1 / R) in space with the edge's image; exactly linear in M (4M / M = 4.0000 in whole counts at every r); the Gauss flux exactly q through every closed surface; the net flow per Node 1 / r^2 in space and 1 / r on the plane; one width [1, 6] (k = count // 6 = 2 D n) gives twice the board's potential within 1 to 2 % for M and 4M at every radius; the digitization k = 0 sets in only where fewer than six units are present. So the pair of nature, the clock as M / r and the force as M / r^2, holds, and by the owner's rule nothing is reverted. Three costs recorded: the field settles in about 12 R^2 intervals (3 000 on 31^3, 40 000 on 121^2) and a change at the source reaches r after about 12 r^2 intervals, not r, so the static field is nature's and the field's response to a moving mass is not; the standing amount grows as q R^2 (667 q on 31^3, 13 040 q on the plane), every cell under 2^30; and the shape holds only where at least three units arrive per Port, a single unit going whole by its momentum (the alternative, back by the largest share, traps it between two Nodes for ever), so a weak source's field is ballistic and 1 / r^2 almost everywhere (a source of 48 per interval on the plane stood seventy times less than Laplace). The reviewer also found that the engine's flow per Node is exactly three times the Gauss flux for a diffusive crowd (a constant of the reading, absorbed in G), and that the two rules of the Node, the coherent sum for a family with a phase circle and the per-Port scatter for one without, are one rule: the scatter is the diagonal of the coherent sum (the weight of a side is the sum of the amounts plus three times the amount through that Port, what the coherent sum gives when the cross terms between Ports vanish), the same numbers to 0.05 %.

The owner, told the single quantum is confirmed ("an event that does not spread and moves in a straight line or stands, which is the remainder": yes, a unit with no whole share goes whole by its momentum or is measured and stands) and that the one-rule form is an improvement for later: "Yes, for later, because everything generic must be replaced by generic; explain this in Highlights." The principle, in the owner's words, as a standing rule of the engine: wherever two code paths do what one generic rule does, the two are replaced by the one, with the same numbers and the same tests, as a change of its own; the engine keeps no special case that a generic rule already covers. Applied here: the scatter of a phase-less family, shipped first as its own function so that the field of matter could be read the same day, becomes the diagonal case of `mix_arrivals` in a following change, with the same integers. Done the same day (the fold, flagged): `scatter_arrivals` is deleted and `mix_arrivals` reads the family's `phase`, the weight of a side for a family without a phase circle being 32^2 x (the sum of the amounts of that number over the six Ports + 3 x the amount of that number through the opposite Port), per number, since arrivals without a phase are mutually incoherent and no cross term survives between numbers either (the diagonal taken over all numbers present steered one number's labels by another's dense field and read the pair's push at 5.96, four times the law; per number the pair reads 1.4486 against the scatter's 1.4488, the product law 3.99996, the asymmetry 0.0085 %); the placement, the leftover whole by the momentum and the leaving phase are one code path for both, the phase-less field runs four times faster (0.07 s per interval on 29^3 against 0.29), and the series C readings move by at most 0.02 %.

The owner, in the same session, asked what the couplings have in common ("maybe from there something generic comes out, like the groups and the sets we have"; "the central idea for the mathematician: every cell performs a simple computation; he should look for transformations from set theory or group theory, and theorems, that help him find patterns"), corrected the count of exits ("there is the Port itself too, is that not 7?": the seventh exit "here" is fixed by every rotation of the cube, so what a Node reads decomposes as two scalars, the presence from outside and what is here, a vector, the net flow, and a traceless tensor of two components that no coupling reads today), and then, told that the board forgets in four places (the largest-remainder placement with its tie order by the tick, the phase-less scatter as a diffusion step, the merge of two measured events on a step and the escape through an open face, and the click), DECIDED: "the board must be time-reversible"; the click at a detector stays the only one-way border, in keeping with "uncertainty is in the detector and not in the board of events" and with the sentence recorded below that cell sensitivity and information remaining on the board will bring Heisenberg. What follows, flagged, for the mathematician's design before any code: the local map of a Node becomes a bijection on its state (every unit's exit a function of its own record and of the coherent sum at the Node, a rotation and not a rounding; the family without a phase circle by a lattice-gas collision rule on the six headings, the seventh exit its rest, with the known defect of the cubic lattice and its remedy named; no merge, two measured events at one Node or the step refused; a reversible board periodic on every axis, an open face being a detector and declared as one; the owed count part of the state, a delay reversible as it stands). This is a change of the law, so it carries a model identity of its own, and the readings of series C and Bell are expected to move by the rounding they lose. The other session's `reversible-detector-v1` (the paragraph below and issue #342) makes a detector's contact invertible on a board without release, suspension, mixing or push; it is kept as a candidate until that design says what of it the one law keeps. The reviews came back the same afternoon (flagged, the orchestrator's record). The architect: a large mass is one phase-less measured event of content M, its far field exactly linear in M and its superposition across numbers exact, no engine change; "millions of Nodes in one Node" is a reading, a coarse board being a world-file rescaling registered per experiment (T = L keeps c), and a coarse Node is a Node; a sun outside the board is a lamp on the face; the real obstacle to a star is the law's whole units (any push gives at least c / 2, any steady diffusive presence halves a clock), left as two decisions for the owner. The mathematician: every coupling is a moment of a declared subset of the arrivals at the Node or a rate off the reader's own clock (already one function, `by_clock`); the seven exits decompose under the cube's 48 symmetries as two scalars (the presence from outside and "here"), a vector (the net flow) and a traceless tensor of two components that no coupling reads; the phase-less scatter is exactly the phase average of the coherent weights; mixing v3 breaks the superposition of two numbers' static fields for a family with a phase circle and keeps it exactly for the phase-less family; and by four theorems no bijective fixed-work rule at the Node can split by the configuration-dependent |c_h|^2 shares or let a lone record diffuse, so a reversible board loses the interference in the placement. Two standalone prototypes then measured it: every unit choosing its exit by its own phase is a bijection (bit-exact return after 300 intervals forward and back) and keeps the lone unit straight and Bell at S = 2, but gives no fringes behind two openings and a fan of rays, 1 / r, in place of the Laplace field; and "everything as rays" (the owner: "check again what happens if everything is rays for us"; "we have a ray and not a wave, does it matter?"; "it may well be that it is received as a wave in the detector"; "why do we have a wave at all?") is bijective at 92 to 100 ns per record with no work at a Node in flight, keeps Bell and the single quantum, and loses two things the coherent sum at the Node was doing: with six headings no screen Node receives rays from both openings (two spots); with rays in 203 directions at one Link per interval the interference term follows the Manhattan path difference (a step, not lambda L / d), and only an isotropic ray clock at speed 1 / 2 in every direction, a rule the engine does not have, gives the two-source fringes (correlation 0.969) in a screen that squares the coherent sum per interval (a plain count never fringes: count_both = count_1 + count_2 exactly); and for rays the presence equals the net flow at every Node, so the clock and the force read the same power of r (M / r and M / r on the plane, M / r^2 and M / r^2 in space, argued), the Einstein pair needing a standing population that only a collision at the Node gives. So the wave at the Node made the lattice isotropic and let a field stand; whether both come back from the record alone (a momentum vector beyond the six Ports with an isotropic flight table, and a lattice-gas collision on the seven slots when rays meet, the seventh "here" the rest state that breaks the cubic lattice's per-line invariant; the owner: "sometimes there are six rays and one more inside the Node") is being measured; the owner has not decided between the wave in the placement and rays with these two additions, and nothing of it is law. Two decisions the owner took meanwhile, on the mathematician's list: (1) ONE READING SET FOR EVERY COUPLING, "everything present at the Node but the reader's own number, including here" (the presence's set today; the push, the size and a detector's threshold read one number today, and a waiting unit counted as presence while a measured event's content did not): a generic law that replaces two reading rules by one, to be implemented with its own test and the series C and Bell readings re-pinned; (2) series C items 1 and 3 (the equivalence 1 : 4 : 16 and the superposition of two fields) are registered as identities of the phase-less field of matter only, since under v3 the fields of a family with a phase circle interfere, and that is the law and not a defect: a correction of the register, not a law. Asked "these are two generic laws, right?": one generic law and one correction of the record. The third prototype (rays with a momentum-like direction and an isotropic flight table, plus a lattice-gas collision on the seven slots when rays meet; the owner: "can the ray be given something that makes it a wave here?") settled the ray question against the rays: the isotropic flight rule gives the fringes (0.969) but the engine's own step rule of a measured event does not (its flight time depends on the vector's representative, not on a distance), so the fringes need a new flight law; the seven-slot collision is an exact bijection (two rest slots needed, 45 000 collisions forward and back bit-exact) but, conserving momentum on six headings, it fires only on head-on pairs, so an outgoing crowd from a source never collides: the field stays ballistic at every density (0.32 / r on the plane, Gauss 1.00), the clock and the force at the same power of r. So a standing field needs a unit to turn back without meeting anything, which no bijective fixed-work rule can do (the mathematician's theorem) and which the scatter of the law does; the pair of Einstein (clock M / r, force M / r^2) and a fully reversible board do not go together in any local rule of fixed work; the only way left, a board filled beforehand with a gas of events in which a mass is a disturbance, is untested research.

The orchestrator's recommendation, flagged, for the owner's decision: the law of today stands (the wave at the Node gives isotropy and the standing field; the board's irreversibility sits in the placement and is the price of the field of matter; the detector stays where the uncertainty is), with three reversible corrections that every path shares: a step onto a measured event no longer merges (refused), an open face is a detector (an escape is a click, the books' escaped line its record), and the clock's owed count is read off the clock by whole division like every other rate (the architect's D2: the smallest slowing is no longer 1 / 2).

The owner: "go with what we talked about, fast", said before the third result; the shared corrections proceed, the deletion of the wave from the Node awaits his word after the result. His word, after the result, 2026-09-19: "Only: there is no wave in the Node. That is the ray."

**DECIDED: the law of the ray.** The Node holds no wave: no coherent sum, no shares, no placement, no tie order, no scatter; a unit is a ray with a record (its Node, an integer momentum vector, its age, its phase in Z_K stamped by its emitter's clock and turned by its family's declared steps per Link, its number, its amount, its content) and moves along the digital line of its momentum at one speed for every direction (at most one Link per interval; 1 / sqrt 3 in space, the phase velocity the wave on the mesh had), a bijection; when rays meet at a Node the seven-slot collision table (the 20 orbits of the 48 symmetries, amount and momentum conserved, two rest slots for "here") permutes their directions, a bijection; a measured event measures by its table and a detector's click adds amplitudes, its record per interval the square of the coherent sum of what arrived (the wave is a reading of a crowd of rays and lives nowhere else); a lamp, and an opening of declared width, re-emit on declared directions with the arriving phase; an open face is a detector; the click is the only one-way border. The price, said twice and accepted: the clock beside a mass reads the presence of rays, which is the flow, so it slows as M / r^2 and not M / r, and the diffusive field of matter of the same day is deleted with the scatter; a standing field would need a board filled with a gas of events, left as research. This is a change of the law and carries a model identity of its own; the design is published before the engine changes, the series C and Bell readings are re-registered under it, and everything of the wave leaves the code and the documents.

The owner named it: the ray is one generic function, `GonenBeam` (the record) and `gonen_beam` (the one function of a Node's interval for the rays present: arrivals, the collision permutation, the measured event's table, the detector's squared record and click, the departures); no other function holds a piece of the ray's law, tables aside.
