The repository's rule that a name states a component's responsibility yields to the owner's explicit name for this one component. And, asked "the tensors and all of it go into generic code too, yes? Every piece of logic once in the code": yes, as a requirement of the implementation. One reading function decomposes the seven slots of a Node (the six Ports and "here", over the one reading set) once into two scalars, a vector and a traceless tensor; every coupling selects its component by a declared key of its table (the clock the scalar, the push the vector, a gravitational-wave detector the tensor, with no new law), and the detector's coherent record is the same function applied to the amplitude vectors of the arrivals, its scalar squared; the collision table is generated from its rule on the 20 orbits, never written by hand; one flight table. No coupling holds a formula of its own; every piece of logic exists once. Implemented the same evening (flagged, the orchestrator's record of what the code does; the design docs/RAY_LAW.md with its section 10 of implementation notes): `events/gonen_beam.py` holds `GonenBeam`, the store of rays, the flight table (c = 1 / sqrt 3, at most one Link per interval), the collision table generated from its classes and checked inverse on every state, `read_arrivals` (the 2 + 3 + 2 decomposition once, the component chosen by a table's `reads`: scalar, outside, here, vector, tensor) and the one function `gonen_beam` with its inverse; `events/measured.py` the measured events and the books; the engine only schedules, books and reads; `mixing.py`, `transit.py` and `reversible.py` are deleted with eighteen tests of the wave, and ten `test_ray_*` modules replace them (the bijection echo, the lone unit, the isotropy of the flight, the collision table's conservation and inverse, the coherent record in phase and in antiphase, re-emission, the face click, the two-slit correlation 0.893 with a 91-direction fan, the clock, the window, the parsing). Re-registered: Bell S = 2 and S' = 3 / 2 exactly, no-signalling exact, 326 criteria; series C 392 criteria, item 1 exact, item 3 the identity, item 7 -Qq / (Mm) exact, Gauss flux and escape 1.0000 q, the front ballistic, item 2 to 5.9 x 10^-6 (the grain of whole apportioning), and nine far-field readings outside the design's expectations registered as measured and not moved (count x r ripples with the lattice ring's Node count, flow x 2 pi r within 0.79 to 1.12): the owner decides whether series C is re-run with a fan source. The clocks on the axis count until the front and then owe the beam's presence, the accepted price. Cost 0.23 us per Node per interval on the plane against 3.6 before. Fourteen implementation decisions in RAY_LAW section 10, among them: the amplitude linear in the amount (32 x amount, for merge invariance), rest rays keep their age, the inverse interval only without measured events, the collision's tie by Port order pinned as one cycle and not proven isotropic on average. The word wave is gone from the code. The physics-rule review of the tables and the readings is still owed.

The mathematician's review of the table of the physical entities (the owner: "run the mathematician on the table of the physical entities; perhaps this too is a group, a set, a tensor that yields the data by our engine or the couplings"), flagged: the table is measured events x families -> (a rule of the chain pass < read < measure < rerelease, a window); in every world of the repository (3 065 cells) no rule differs from what the arrival's keys give (read for a free ray, measure for a paid one), the forty declared entries being windows; the keys form three live cells and `kind` follows from `quantum` (h = 0 free, h >= 1 paid); the push is one bilinear form of rank three, kappa = -M (x) 1 for gravity (the emitter's vector the constant 1, the equivalence principle), q (x) q / M for the electric part, 1 (x) h s for the paid family, on the vector component of the one reading; the clock's form is of rank one and blind to the family; a tensor detector is a column key of a table entry, not a family; a second circle or a handedness is a new field of the record with its own Z_2, not generated by the present keys; one residual site is not generic, the electric push's lookup of the emitter's charge and content by number.

The owner, 2026-09-19: "I approve 1 and 3, and 2 with the physicist": (1) the tables are generated from the keys and a world declares only what differs (windows, thresholds, lamps, a `reads` component), `kind` derived from `quantum`; (3) the one reading function is the amount-weighted moments of order 0, 1 and 2 of the direction vectors, valid for fans as for the six headings, "here" entering the zeroth moment alone (its direction is zero); (2) the push as one bilinear form with the emitter's factor carried on the ray's record, to be implemented only with the physics-rule review of the ray law, which is now dispatched together with it. Asked what the model has and whether numbers of nature can be derived, the orchestrator answered (flagged): exact identities (Gauss, equivalence, the Coulomb form, no-signalling, the books) and readings (Bell S = 2, the fringes in the record), but no number of nature yet, since the constants are inputs of the world file, there are no bound states (any push gives at least c / 2 for ever, the architect's D1) and the clock reads M / r^2; the first derivable number would be a ratio between two orbits around a mass once a push can give a small speed.

The owner, 2026-09-19: "1. Try. In parallel check what code can be cleaned. 2. The ray is to be called NatureBeam in the code everywhere. 3. Make sure there is optimization in everything. Make smart decisions." So: the ray's record is `NatureBeam` and the one function `nature_beam` (replacing the name GonenBeam given earlier the same day; the naming exception in RAY_LAW moves to the new name); D1 is tried as a world key (a width S with the step one Link per (S M + p) / p self-creations per axis, S = 1 today, the smallest speed 1 / (S + 1)) with an orbit experiment on the plane registered as series D; a cleanup and a performance pass over the whole engine follow the physics review. Done the same night (flagged, the orchestrator's record): (1) the table of a measured event is generated from the families' keys (`world.default_table`) and a world declares only what differs, `kind` derived from `quantum` and its key refused; the one reading `read_arrivals` is the amount-weighted moments of order 0, 1 and 2 of the arrivals' direction vectors (the tensor 3 M2 - tr M2 I, exact integers), valid for fans; every example world parses as before and the Bell and coupling runs are unchanged record by record. (2) The rename to NatureBeam / nature_beam. (3) D1 tried: the world key `width` S (default 1, the law as it was), the step one Link per (S M + p) / p self-creations, and series D, the orbit, on the plane: a fixed source releasing on a fan of 120 directions and a probe with the tangential momentum derived for a circular orbit at S = 1, 8, 32 and r = 12, 24. In the first run one orbit closed (S = 32, r = 12: 346 intervals against 343 derived, an eccentric loop) and the grain of whole units broke the rest; re-run under the moments, no orbit closes at any width, and the reason is a finding for the owner: a fan ray's push is its label content x amount x D and |D| of a fan direction is 5.19 on average, so equal amounts on (1, 0, 0) and (7, 5, 0) carry momenta 1 and sqrt 74; the law as designed, registered as open; the orchestrator recommends the label along the unit vector at the flight table's scale Q = 64 (one table), for the owner's ruling. (4) The physics-rule review of the law of the ray: the flight and collision tables pass (c = 1 / sqrt 3 within +0.77 % by direction; the collision's tie a fixed 3-cycle of the axes, 4.2 % of the (state, symmetry) pairs non-equivariant, accepted); the clock, the record (a linear amplitude: a classical coherent meter, no Born rule) and Bell hold; the nine far-field readings of series C are the ring estimator's lattice geometry, reproduced exactly from r / Nodes(r), the ring sums exact (count / q = 1.000 at every r), no fan re-run needed; two blocking findings fixed under the standing authorization: the push, the click and the re-emission read the momentum by the arrival Port and not by the record's label (now one label, `momentum_labels`, everywhere), and the record's amplitude products were unchecked (now the affordable amount 261123 per detector Node per family per interval, refused beyond); and the orchestrator's decision under "make smart decisions": no collision at a Node holding a measured event (rays meet the table, not each other), which also settles the threshold's set as the arrivals. (5) Proposal 2 implemented as the physicist admitted it: the push is one bilinear form, kappa = -M_A for a free ray, + q_A q_B / M_B as sign x by_clock(age, |V q_A q_B|, M_B) for a charged free ray, + 1 for a paid ray, on the label moment; (q_B, M_B) carried on the ray's record from birth as two integers; the lookup of the emitter by number is deleted; series 7 reads integer by integer as before (kappa 0 in 7_pp and 7_mm). (6) The cleanup: the deleted disturbance contract marked as history in four skills and the definitions, eight dead functions of core/integer.py and MIXING_OPPOSITE deleted, numpy declared, the version 0.3.1, the package import lazy. The performance pass (the audit measured: the per-measured-event loop 60 % of the two-slit interval, the dense readings 16 %, the sort key, the books; projected 0.14 us per Node on the plane and 1.2 us per ray on the slits) is next. Done (flagged): five bit-exact optimizations (the measured events' interval in bulk, the dense readings on request over the active Nodes, the merge by one packed key, the books as running ledger lines with a recount asserted equal, the cached collision table, the clocks' frame in bulk), every one of the 45 example worlds byte-identical before and after: the plane 0.20 to 0.11 us per Node per interval, the two slits 2.43 to 0.69 us per ray, the suite 16.8 to 5.5 s. And one bug of the night fixed under the standing authorization: the record's affordable amount (261123) refused `two_contents` at tick 19 (262144 units at one face Node in one interval); a detector's record is a report of the host and not the law's local work, so it is now exact and never refused (the pointer in the register, its square and the cumulative record in Python's unbounded integers), 44 worlds byte-identical and `two_contents` completing its 200 intervals with a face record of 181 x 2^62.

The owner, asked whether the label's magnitude had been checked with the physicist (it had not; it is sent to him before any ruling) and whether the external sources behave like the detector: "They inherit code from it. There is something important in it, and that is the sensitivity: the uncertainty principle comes from not being able to measure a single Node, as it were, or something like that; say a detector measuring three Nodes sees one electron that can be on any of the three."

Recorded as a principle of the detector: a detector is a set of Nodes with ONE record; a click says "here, in one of these" and not which; the declared width is the position's uncertainty and the reading, not the board, is what is uncertain. What follows for the engine (the orchestrator, flagged): the detector's coherent pointer, its record and its threshold are taken over the whole set of its Nodes (one pointer per detector per family per interval; the threshold on the arrivals summed over the set; the click naming the detector), not per Node as implemented tonight; a source, an opening and a screen are the same object with the same width key; and the Heisenberg run A10 the owner asked for at the start (an opening of width w as a re-emitting detector set, the product of its width and the momentum spread read on the screen, over w) is registered as the test of it.

The owner, the same hour: "In the code a ray is defined as NatureBeam? It reads, as it were, a 'quantum state'. On the board there are only events. It must also return to the board the information it received." Recorded: the coherent pointer over a detector's set is a reading and the only place a "state" exists, the board holding rays alone; and what a detector receives goes back to the board: the content (it joins the measured event and quickens its clock, E = h f), the momentum (the recoil and the push, one label) and, decided now, the PHASE: after a click the measured event's own phase takes the set's pointer phase, so what it emits afterwards carries what it received (the pointer of the reversible detector absorbed as the detector's phase; a pass or a read leaves the phase alone; the clock's turn by content over K unchanged). "So its draw is in the quantum state": yes; the board draws nothing (a bijection), and the click is the one many-to-one map, many records in and one result out ("here, in one of these", at the pointer's phase), which is what looks random from outside though every step is determined; the limit stated with it: that reading is a classical coherent sum in one detector, so Bell reads S = 2 and not 2 sqrt 2, since a record of one detector may not depend on another's (no-signalling), the model's own prediction and not a defect. Asked "do we have a wave function for the engine's code?" and "why in the detector is it not a ray too? or did you use a physical law?": on the board none; the one wave-like object is the detector's coherent pointer over its set, a reading born and dead in one interval; and yes, the coherent sum with its square is an IMPORTED law of physics (intensity as the square of the summed amplitudes), the only imported law in the engine, brought in because a count of rays never gives fringes; the alternative on whole rays alone is a pairing rule in the detector (a ray clicks unless a ray of opposite phase, within the window, arrives at the set in the same interval; the pair passes on), which gives fringes as a triangle where the sum gives a cosine, the same limit Bell showed.

The owner, 2026-09-19: "Put it as a parameter of the detector. They are wave or beam. Beam the default."

**DECIDED: the detector key `reading`, "beam" by default (the pairing rule on whole rays, no amplitude, no square, the record a count) and "wave" (the coherent sum, the imported law, kept as an option and marked as such); A10 is run under both.** Asked how many places carry the name NatureBeam and whether another name remains: 22 NatureBeam and 106 nature_beam in 25 files, no old name left in the code, the tests, the tools or the examples (Highlights keeps the history); the identifiers RayWorld, RaySimulation, RayStore and RayTables and the common noun ray remain for the world, the run, the store and the tables of the rays, to be renamed on the owner's word: "Yes", 2026-09-19; they become NatureBeamWorld, NatureBeamSimulation, NatureBeamStore, NatureBeamTables and their functions parse_nature_beam_world, execute_nature_beam_run, is_nature_beam_world, nature_beam_tables, the test modules test_nature_beam_*, after the running detector work lands; the law's identity in the world file (`"law": "rays"`, `rays-v1`) is data and stays, as does the common noun ray.

**The physicist's verdict on the label's magnitude (2026-09-19, flagged):** content x amount x D is a defect of the fan extension and not a hypothesis (|p| depends on the integer representative of the direction, is discontinuous in it and diverges under a refinement of the direction table; "magnitude as today" was written for |D| = 1); the label along the unit vector at the flight table's scale, u_d the integer vector nearest Q D / |D| with Q = 64 by an exact integer rounding rule (no tie for any direction bound below 147, the headings exactly Q e_d), is admissible with four corrections: the exact rounding rule with the sign restored; the step rule by_clock(age, |p|, Q S M + |p|), which keeps `width`'s meaning and every registered step bit-identical; the parser's and the weights' bound at Q x content x amount (2^56 per row, the registered worlds at most 2^24); and the reading's vector and tensor moments on u_d too. Exact under it: every momentum transfer, the books, the collision table, antisymmetry, the 48 symmetries, the bijection, no remainder at any Node; approximate at the table's grain: |u_d| = Q within 1.35 % and the label's direction within 0.8 degrees, comparable to the flight's own 0.77 %. Series C's pushes and momenta scale by 64 exactly, the clock, the threshold and Gauss unchanged, Bell unchanged; series D re-derived expects a closed orbit at S = 32 within the grain. The owner, told it only fixes things: yes; it proceeds after the detector work, and series D runs again. The clock beside a mass (the accepted price, M / r^2): the owner asked for a solution, "it must be checked in the detector and not on the board", and one exists inside the law: the ray's AGE, the count of intervals since the measured event that created it (a birth or a re-emission; a collision is a permutation and cannot reset it, or the board would stop being a bijection), is on the record and travels with it, so a clock reading the amount-weighted age of its arrivals (a first moment in the age, the one reading function with `reads: "age"`) reads (M / r^2) x r = M / r in space while the push keeps reading the flow, M / r^2: Einstein's pair from two readings of the same rays, no estimator, fixed work, additive over sources like Newton's potential (the owner's questions and the answers: locality holds since the ray counted its own age and the Node learned nothing it was not brought; the merge of identical rows keeps the sum since equal rows have equal ages; interference does not enter, the clock reads amounts and ages without phase; the ray's other fields carry no distance, and the phase is the same counter read modulo K, so a family without a phase circle is one whose counter is read whole; the dilution of the fan is the 1 / r^2 itself and a decaying ray would go the wrong way; in space the shell has 4 pi r^2 Nodes, so r^2 and not r^3). Costs: the age kept whole instead of modulo the direction's period (the store bounded by the board's diameter on an open board; a world key for the bound on a periodic one), a host cost. The owner, 2026-09-19: "Go for it. Perhaps this is how the returning message from an event knows where to stop": recorded as an idea, a ray sent back toward its source could count its age down and be measured where it reaches zero; not implemented. The owner's questions the same hour and the answers, recorded: the age is a property of the record and not of an event (an event is a step and its age is 1; the record counts the events it has been through, as the phase already does for light); only a measured event resets it, at a birth, a re-emission (a new record) or a click (the record ends), and a step, a collision, a wait or a periodic crossing never do; the board writes the counter and uses it only modulo the direction's period for the flight, and only a measured event, the external thing, reads it whole. "Great, this does not change the physics of the world on the board": exactly; the board is unchanged and one reading is added at the gate, the day's principle that the forces are what the detector measures. And the placement in the code, the owner's instruction: the age reading lives in an external place, outside the board's law, with the explanation that it only helps the detector's computation; "this converts the game board into the world of the detector, which is our world": the transformation the owner asked for in the morning has one form now, the one reading function at the gate, moments of the records that arrived (the scalar the presence, the age the potential, the vector the force, the tensor a gravitational wave, the amplitude the interference), the board holding rays alone and our world being what those moments say. "So also in the detector no formulas are allowed, right?": right, and nearly so already: the detector computes sums of what arrived (amount, amount x direction, the second moment, amount x age, the amplitude vectors) and Newton, Einstein and Coulomb are results of those sums, not formulas in the code; what remains formula-like is the coupling, the reader's content times the flow plus q_A q_B / M_B times the flow (one division), and the optional `wave` reading's square. The owner, 2026-09-19: "Go for it: charge per unit of content. Our charge is per family, right?" (it is: the family declares the whole charge of its measured events, an event may override it).

**DECIDED: `charge` is the charge per unit of content of a family, an integer or a pair [n, d] as `suspension` is (series 7 needs 1 / 2), a measured event's charge is that times its content and a per-event charge is refused; the ray's record carries no charge and no mass columns (the family suffices; the two columns added tonight go); the push is one product, M_A x (rho_A rho_B - 1) x the flow for a free ray, the electric part read off the clock by whole division of the declared pair as every rate is; the equivalence principle then holds for the electric push as for gravity.** To be implemented after the three changes in flight land.

**The Heisenberg run A10 (2026-09-20; examples/events/heisenberg, EXPERIMENTS A10, the page for the owner):** an opening of w = 1, 3, 9, 27 Nodes declared as one re-emitting detector, a screen 108 Links behind read as pixels; the count never narrows (rms of sin theta 0.32 at every w), the `wave` record narrows with w (0.319 to 0.224) and at w = 27 reads w x FWHM = 4.99 against the law's 0.886 lambda = 4.09; at w = 3 and 9 the fan of 47 directions is too sparse for the lobe to form (a limit of the reading, registered); the `beam` record gives no bound (the pairing cancels a third of the clicks at w = 27 and what survives keeps the fan's spread). Asked "why is there no Heisenberg?": because it is a property of a sum, the cancellation of the w emitters' rays off the axis, and `beam` counts whole rays and never sums; asked what it helps: to be right about light (the fringes and the fan are measured facts; without the sum the model predicts neither), while the atom's stability and its levels come from the board and not from it; "so the wave belongs in the screen? because in the world behind the detector one sees only waves and not rays": exactly, nature is seen through detectors of a width, an interval and a threshold, and what they deliver is a sum of many rays, which behaves as a wave; a detector narrow and sensitive enough to catch one ray sees a click, a particle: wave and particle are the width and the threshold of the detector, not properties of light.

**DECIDED (the owner, 2026-09-20):** `wave` is the default reading of a detector, `beam` a declared option; Bell must read S = 2 unchanged under it (one ray per interval, the pointer is the ray).

**THE PRINCIPLE, the owner, 2026-09-20:** "So the mathematics works out: on the game board a ray, and in the world a wave is received." Confirmed by the measurements of the day and recorded as the standing statement of the model: on the board a ray with a record, one law, a bijection, no remainder (the echo forward and back bit-exact); at the gate a sum of the rays that arrived (the one reading function); in the world behind the detector a wave (A10: the record narrows with the width of the opening while the count never does; the two openings: the fringes in the record and never in the count). The label along the unit vector at Q = 64 landed the same hour with the physicist's four corrections (series C identical in its readings, Bell unchanged), and series D under it: at S = 32 and r = 12 the probe stays bound for 2 891 intervals through seven turns (period 289 against 343 derived, the mean radius 11.25) without returning to one Link of its start; the field's bursts and the lattice's grain remain the named causes. The reading of the audit of issue #361 (2026-09-20), adopted and flagged: the principle is a statement about a crowd, not about light in general; a wave is the sum of the events that reach one detector set in one interval, so a single quantum per interval interferes with nothing, and at a low rate the model predicts additive counts, which nature's single-particle experiments contradict (HYPOTHESES section 11; A10 at a low rate measures it). The wave is in the crowd, not in the event; the principle is the model's limit there, stated so that a reader does not take it as a claim about every light.

**The architecture review after the night (2026-09-20, the skill applied in full, flagged):** RAY_LAW sections 2 to 5 exist once each where the plan puts them and the notes are consistent; ownership holds; the GENERICITY probe passed on four active worlds (Bell, coupling 7_mp, an entity world, the two slits: every label renamed to keywords and prototype names and every list reversed, families, numbers, directions, detectors, entries: states, rows, books and events equal 4 of 4; the only order that matters is a measured event's own declared `directions`, the documented tie of `apportion_whole`); three blocking defects, each one function, fixed under the standing authorization: a born label's product formed in int64 and checked after it (a wrap inside the bound passed silently), a free ray re-emitted by a charged event of another family stamped with mass 0 so a charged reader divided by zero at tick 15 of a certified world, and the gravity push reading the reader's content inside the per-family loop after earlier families' clicks joined it, so the world's family order changed the integers; decided by the orchestrator under "make smart decisions": the push reads the content the frame read, once, order-independent; a re-emitted ray carries nothing of its emitter once the charge is per unit of content (the family suffices); also found: five documents name `push_form`, a function the code does not have; the tools replay three rules of the engine (a second owner, to be made to read the engine's own functions); ARCHITECTURE's dependency table and ENTITY_DEFINITIONS' wording are stale; a detector may be named `face:+x` (to be refused); and 23 name redundancies, tabled in the glossary of 5.6 with the one name recommended for each, adopted.

**The principles of the law of events, and what follows for Highlights and the engine (model owner, 2026-09-19, the same day).** The model owner gave a text of fifteen principles, formulated in another conversation, and asked that it be checked against what was decided ("See that with this there are no contradictions between what we discussed"). The orchestrator found one contradiction, the fixing mechanism of its points 8 to 12, and the owner ruled: "So we do not want a return to the source. If there are no contradictions, record all the principles and what follows from them for the change of Highlights and the engine." The principles, given in Hebrew and translated, in the text's numbering, with the owner's decision on each: (1) The board is a system of physical information. It does not only move particles: it develops the physical state, carries information between Nodes and makes events. Matter, the detectors and the clocks are part of the board; there is no external manager that arranges the results. Adopted. (2) Every development must obey the local physical laws. Paths, headings and changes are not arbitrary: they are set by the content that reaches a Node, its state and the allowed couplings. What we call fields and forces must be expressed in these local responses, not in an influence the computer adds from afar. Adopted. (3) From an event leave continuations that carry its information. The spreading on the board carries content, phase and the link to the previous event, through neighbouring Nodes, at the speed the model defines and in the headings the physical law allows. Adopted. (4) At the minimum unit there is no further split. When a single portion that cannot be divided is received, it continues as a ray in one direction and does not divide into fractions that stay stuck. Its heading can change in a physical interaction. The relation between this numerical unit and a physical photon still had to be defined. Adopted; the relation is in "A single quantum" below. (5) The source and the detector are not two separate kinds of physics. What emits can also absorb and respond. The emission itself is a local change in the source, and the source must take part in the account of content and recoil, not make a photon from outside without paying. Adopted. (6) An observed event is a partial fixing. The record fixes a certain result at a certain place and time; it does not reveal the whole state or fix a single past path. Yet the result can also constrain possibilities in other parts coordinated with it. Adopted with the orchestrator's reading, flagged: the path was one, since a quantum is at one Node in every interval and nothing is random; the record does not reveal it, the audit display can; and a result constrains other parts only through the board, by the events that leave the fixing. (7) The detector does not peek into the board. It responds through its local interaction and does not read the list of possibilities, the whole content of the Node or remote information. Our audit display can show more than the detector itself can measure. Adopted. (8 to 12) The fixing mechanism: an arrival makes a candidate for fixing and not yet an observed result, the content waiting at the last Node until a confirmation arrives; a returning quantum, single, carrying the new information back to the previous event's Node to report; an update whose purpose is to prevent a double or contradictory realization; confirmation and update subject to locality and time; what is not decided going on developing in accord with what is. Not adopted: "So we do not want a return to the source." Under the law of events a whole quantum is at one Node in every interval and is measured once, where it is, so there is no double realization to prevent, nothing to confirm and nothing to report, and a candidate waiting is a store at a Node, which the law forbids. What 11 and 12 also say, that an influence reaches the rest only through the board and that a physical record in a detector suffices to define the observed event, the law says already. (13) Nothing disappears into nothing, but there can be absorption: a photon can stop existing as a separate photon when its energy and momentum pass to the absorbing body; it does not follow that a quantum must leave in the opposite direction; if a returning ray leaves, it, the body's recoil and the source of its energy are computed too. Adopted; under the law of events a returning ray would be a release by the measured event's table, paid from its content, and none is wanted. (14, 15) The conclusions of the checks: in the check model, with laws entered explicitly, a single ray reached a detector, absorption conserved energy and momentum and so did back-scatter with recoil, an isolated check and not a proof that the mechanism follows from Universe24; the central connection, one local law for quantum propagation and interference, candidacy, return and confirmation and a single consistent realization, is not demonstrated, and in the tested code the minimum unit still stuck in remainders, its continuation as a ray an experimental addition. Recorded as the state of the checks; the stuck remainders are the engine of the law of the shadow, which the law of events replaces. The text's closing sentence, "The board develops physical information by local laws; measurement fixes part of it as a record; the update mechanism should ensure that the related development respects the fixing, without double realization and without breaking the laws of physics", reads under the law of events without its third clause: the fixing is a measurement, the merging of the entering record into the measured event's, and the update is the continuations the measured event releases afterwards, carrying its number, its clock and its momentum; nothing else updates anything. What follows for Highlights, the orchestrator's derivation, flagged, each item for the owner to strike: readings (b) and (e) of the law of events are amended as recorded there; the open decision of 5.5 on a returned field quantum is closed, nothing returns; the third law is paid at emission and at measurement; and the clock: by "the event carries" and principle 1 (the clocks are part of the board), the clock of an event is in its record, its age, the intervals since its birth or its measurement, and every rate of a measured event, its release n / d per Port and its phase content / K, is read off its age by whole division, so that a measured event of small content releases seldom but releases and no remainder exists anywhere; the phase turn of a family in transit is a declared whole number of steps of the circle per Link, the family's frequency, since events in transit merge at Nodes and carry no age of their own; for the owner to confirm. A single quantum (the orchestrator's definition at the owner's request, "Define for me now a single quantum", flagged): the unit of the amount, the smallest whole that moves, an event of amount 1; an event of a larger amount is a bundle of quanta of one number moving with one record. Its record: amount 1, phase, number (the measured event that released it, the last emitter), momentum (from birth, the recoil the emitter took, its direction), its heading now, and its counts (the suspension written on it at an exit, and its age). It is at one Node in every interval and at one neighbour in the next, or at the same Node while suspended, counting for itself and delaying what is behind it at that exit. It goes by its momentum: alone at a Node, to the neighbour in its momentum's heading; in a crowd of its number, it is one of the units the sides' shares place and may leave by another side, keeping its momentum and returning to its line at the next lone Node, the turn a step's detour and not a change of what it is. It never splits, spreads, parks, leaves a remainder or loses information, even as the only one of its number on the board. It is seen only by measurement: entering a Node with a measured event whose table says measure for its family, it becomes measured there, one click at one place in one interval, its amount joining, its momentum entering, its phase read. A bundle spreads at a crowded Node because the shares reach wholes; a single quantum never; the wave is many, the particle is one, and there are not two laws. The key `quantum` of a family is no longer a threshold assembled at a holder but the content one unit of a paid family costs its emitter and gives what measures it, the exchange rate between the families' units. What follows for the engine, the orchestrator's list, flagged (the engine of the law of events replaces the engine of the law of the shadow: one engine, one branch, tests of the code only, "without experiments"): one kind of thing, the event with its record, in transit (the arrays per Node, number and Port, without the parked layer) or measured (the events fixed at Nodes with their tables, what was the holder, its record counting its clicks); the Node's interval: read what is present through the tables without names, the sides' shares from the vectors (the coherent sum, the leaving amplitudes, the squared sizes, kept from the mixing), wholes per side, the leftover whole by the momentum's heading, the momentum of a bundle divided among the sides exactly within the interval; the exit per Port, its suspension derived from the sizes read and written on the event, the order on a lane kept and the event behind a suspended one delayed; measurement by the table, the record merging; release by the measured event's table and its rate off its clock, with recoil; deleted: the parked ninths and their release, the wait's debt and hold, the turn's remainder, the register of `quantum` and every remainder of a measured event; the books: in transit, measured and escaped per family, and the momentum over all records, balanced at every interval; the world file: the law selected by the name events, the measured events and the events in transit at the start, the families with their tables, ρ, K, N, the suspension's width, the steps per Link per family and the exchange rate per family, the names fixed in the engine's contract; the word shadow leaves the code with the engine. What the tests show is in 5.5.

**Detector as physical Nodes; cell sensitivity and information (model owner, 2026-09-19, later discussion).** The owner clarified, translated from Hebrew: "The detector is itself Nodes"; "The important thing is the detector sensitivity"; a result can say that an event was in a group of Nodes, with the coverage generic rather than fixed to three; "I think that cell sensitivity and information having to remain on the board will bring Heisenberg." The requested implementation separates coverage, distinguishable output groups and a quantum threshold: three covered Nodes do not require three quanta. The detector belongs to the board and produces a physical common output through local interactions; the observer only reads it. Complete board state is definite, while the observed record can be coarse. The final sentence is the owner's research hypothesis, not a derived uncertainty relation or an output constraint. The earlier one-way `events-v1` measurement boundary remains its historical and default contract; the stronger on-board information requirement is implemented only through an explicitly selected candidate, never silently claimed of the old absorption rule. The orchestrator's concrete proposal, flagged as an assumed local law rather than an owner's microscopic formula: `reversible-detector-v1`, controlled addition into an ordinary measured Event's phase and invertible contact routing with local recoil, original carriers retained, bounded no-escape trials, finite physical output and no reset. Its exact domain, inverse, units, shared-readout encoding and unproved quantum targets are owned by [the detector contract](DETECTOR_REQUIREMENTS.md#implementation-contract-reversible-detector-v1). It does not reintroduce mandatory backward emission, a two-Node coincidence condition, an oracle or hidden registers.

**Separate entity definitions (model owner, 2026-09-19, clarification).** Translated from Hebrew: "The detector settings are in an entity definitions file, not in the engine; make sure the workflow is in the appropriate skill." The orchestrator's implementation reading: reusable material geometry, local-rule selections and detector groups are authored in an external data file and placed by an explicit world reference. A canonical host loader resolves this into ordinary on-board Events before the generic engine runs; external file ownership does not mean off-board physical information or a correction oracle. The exact file, placement, portable-input and provenance contract is [Reusable entity definitions](ENTITY_DEFINITIONS.md), and development follows the linked field-development workflow. This corrects the earlier inline-world-only scope assumption without changing the selected local physical law.

**DECIDED: the name of the board is GameBoard (the owner, 2026-09-20).** The owner, asked to explain "why in the world a wave and on the game board a ray", added: "that is what it is to be called in English in every place, also in the code: GameBoard". Recorded as the canonical name of the physical lattice of Nodes: the GameBoard, `GameBoard` in code, `game_board` in module and function names; "board", "lattice" and "grid" are not to be used for it in the active simulator, its tests, tools and documents (as `Site` is not used for a Node). The GameBoard is the cubic lattice of Nodes of Highlights 3.1 with the six Port headings in Port order and the faces per axis, open or periodic; a world declares its `shape`. The rename rides the NatureBeam rename, one pull request, with the terminology entry and the migration note.

The orchestrator's answer to "why", recorded, flagged for the owner to strike: on the GameBoard a NatureBeam is one event at one Node with one direction, one phase and whole numbers; it never splits and the interval is a bijection, so nothing at a Node ever holds a sum of two paths, and a single ray cannot interfere with itself. A detector is a set of Nodes with one record, and its `wave` reading is the pointer P = sum over the arrivals of a_k (cos phi_k, sin phi_k); its square is sum a_k^2 + 2 sum a_j a_k cos(phi_j - phi_k). The first term is the count (the `beam` reading); the second is the interference, present only where one record sums more than one ray with a definite phase difference, which is the path difference in Links times the family's phase per Link. The wave is therefore a reading of a crowd of rays by a set, and it exists in the world because every instrument in the world (an eye, a screen, an antenna) is such a set, reading many rays over many intervals; no instrument reads one ray at one Node in one interval, which is the uncertainty. The measurements of the day stand as the evidence: the count never narrows and the `wave` record narrows with the width (A10); the fringes of series C are in the record and not on the GameBoard; the age reading converts the GameBoard's intervals into the world's clock (M/r) without touching the GameBoard.

**Series E, the clock's redshift in space, and the age reading landed (2026-09-20, the implementation and the run).** The age is kept whole on the record and read whole only by a measured event (the flight reads it modulo the period, a collision never); the one reading gains the age moment `sum amount x age`, chosen by `reads: "age"` on a table entry, and the clock of such an entry counts it in place of the presence; the world key `age_bound` bounds the age (twice the flight bound by default on a board with an open axis, required on a board periodic on every axis, a ray beyond it refuses the run). Every world without the key reads the same integer by integer; the board's step is unchanged (the test with 324 colliding rays over 40 intervals: Node, direction, phase, amount and content identical every interval, only the ages differ). Series E (a 31^3 open cube, a source of 2^12 at the centre on 290 directions, probes of content 1 on whole shells r = 4 .. 14): the scalar clock reads k x r^2 = 39 .. 45 and the age clock k x r = 33 .. 39, the pair M / r^2 and M / r from the same rays, the ratio of the constants 0.870 against sqrt 3 / 2 = 0.866 (the age at r is r sqrt 3); the redshift between the age clocks has the form of the 1 / r law (the nearer clock slower), 11 readings inside the brackets and 3 outside on a field that is not weak (k of 2 .. 9); a weak-field run is the follow-up. A single probe on an axis reads its line's beam, as predicted: the shell means are the law's readings.

**The physicist on hydrogen, atoms and nuclei (2026-09-20, flagged, for the owner's decisions).** Hydrogen as two free families, the proton (content 1836, charge per unit of content +1) and the electron (content 1, charge per unit of content -15): the couplings are right (every `read` record shows -(1 + ratio) m Q per ray along u_d; the opposite charge stays, the uncharged and the like-charged leave), and the three ratios of electric to gravity (ee, ep, pp) come out in nature's proportions 1836^2 : 1836 : 1 from the contents alone; the scale is a declared number. The closed orbit is prevented by the fan's grain at one Node (0 to 8 rays per shell around a mean of 4 at r = 8; the in-plane electron read 57 % of the ring mean, drifted out to r = 27 and escaped after 1.7 turns; tilted out of the plane it read the sphere's flux within 7 % and made 1.6 bound turns), not by the law's form. The fix the report names is the owner's own principle: the electron as a measured event on a set of width 3 (one record), which averages the +-100 % of one Node to +-20 %; series F is to be re-run with it. Quantization: nothing in the law selects radii and the orbit does not decay (a free ray carries no content), so the atom is a classical continuum; Bohr's radii would need the body's phase to turn by its momentum label per Link stepped plus a closure reading against its own past, for which the law has no place, a law change. Nuclei: two protons have kappa = M (rho^2 - 1), zero at rho = 1 and repulsion above; a proton and a neutron bind by gravity only; no two-proton nucleus binds where the atom is electric; the strong force is not in the law. The report's proposal: a second density column sigma with the attractive sign on a family of its own whose rays are booked as escaped at a declared age L (a range of L Links, formula-free, local), binding at one Link for sigma^2 > rho^2 - 1; no binding energy or mass defect exists in the law. Helium waits on a bound nucleus and on quantization. Open for the owner: (1) the electron as a set of width 3 and series F again; (2) the sigma column, a new physical hypothesis needing its own model identity; (3) Bohr's turn rule, the same.

**The proposals for the strong and the weak force and for Bohr, and the decision on Bohr (the owner, 2026-09-20).** The owner asked whether there is a coupling for the strong and the weak nuclear force ("that is what holds protons, no?") and whether Bohr's orbits "should come out of the frequency as a whole number on the orbit". The orchestrator's answers, flagged: the law has one coupling, M_A (rho_A rho_B - 1) x flow, so two protons of rho = 1 read zero and above it repel; the strong force (attractive, one or two Links, stronger than the electric there, saturating) is not in the law, and the weak force holds nothing, it turns one family into another (a decay). Bohr's whole number is de Broglie's: momentum x the length of the orbit = j h, a whole number of the phase's turns along the path, not of the clock's ticks in time; the physicist checked the owner's formulation with the clock (T / K = j) and with Kepler it gives r_j ~ j^(2/3), not j^2. The seven proposals tabled for the owner: (1) a body on a set of Nodes with one record (the electron of width 3; an engine feature, not a law), (2) the strong force as a second density column sigma with the attractive sign, kappa = M_A (rho_A rho_B - sigma_A sigma_B - 1) x flow, (3) its range as a family of sigma rays booked as escaped at a declared age L, (4) the weak force as a table entry that re-releases a measured event as another family, (5) Bohr's turn rule: at every Link a body steps its phase turns by its momentum label, one declared world constant `action` (h), a whole turn per step with the remainder read off the clock, (6) Bohr's closure as a reading: a detector's `wave` record of the rays the electron releases over many turns adds coherently only for an orbit whose phase closes, so the lines appear in the record while the board allows every radius, (7) a feedback from the record to the board so that only closed orbits survive on the board (not recommended). The owner: "Bohr should come out by itself behind the detector; what is missing on the board by the laws?" The answer, flagged: one thing, on the body and not on the detector: a measured event's phase turns only by its own clock (content / K per self-creation), by time alone, so the rays it releases carry to the detector how long and not how far; on the orbit there is no wavelength for the detector to read, and what comes out by itself is the closure in time, j^(2/3). What is missing is the tie between a body's momentum and its phase that E = h f already gives a released ray; with it the detector shows Bohr by itself in the existing `wave` reading, nothing is kept at a Node, the interval stays a bijection and the board allows every radius. DECIDED (the owner): "On Bohr, go, and put it as parameters outside the board like the age." Recorded as the placement rule: the turn is a declared parameter of the measured event, the external thing, read by it from its own record (its momentum label and the Links it stepped, derived from its age by the step rule as the owed count is) with no memory at a Node; the rays' flight and collision are untouched; a world without the key reads the same integer by integer. The body on a set (1) is taken with it as the condition for a closed orbit; proposals 2, 3 (the strong force) await the owner's decision; 4 and 7 are recorded only.

**DECIDED: series G, the Hubble diagram behind the detector (the owner, 2026-09-20).** Asked what remains to decide (the strong force; series G; the scale of the constants; which recorded-only items to queue), the owner chose "2: let it check whether what is observed today is also seen in our detector." Recorded as the question of series G: sources thrown from a centre with a spread of momenta, each a family of its own releasing rays with its phase; a detector at the centre reads, per source, the redshift (the rate of the pointer's turn in its record against the emitter's own rate) and the distance (the age of the arriving rays, the flight time); the curve of redshift against distance is compared in shape with what is observed today (the linear law near, and at large distance the supernova curve of an accelerating recession, the deceleration parameter about -0.55) and with the two curves the law can give, a coasting recession (no push, the Milne form) and a decelerating one (the push of the crowd). The orchestrator's expectation, flagged, stated before the run: the cosmological redshift is not on the board (a ray's phase per Link is fixed and a collision does not touch it); the detector reads only the Doppler of the recession and the clocks' rates, so the Hubble law comes out by itself from the throw and nothing in the law gives an acceleration; the age clock is the one reading that could bend the curve (an emitter's clock read the age moment of a younger crowd when the far light left), and whichever way it bends is to be reported as measured, not tuned. The strong force (proposals 2 and 3) stays open; the scale of the constants stays "proportions at a smaller scale" until a measurement needs more; no recorded-only item is queued.

**The owner's answers on the scale of the constants, the queue and the strong force (2026-09-20).** On the scale of the constants: "3, fine; is this ratio that came out for us worth a paper?" The orchestrator's answer, flagged: no; the three ratios of electric to gravity in the proportions 1836^2 : 1836 : 1 follow from the form of the one coupling, M_A (rho_A rho_B - 1) x flow with rho the charge per unit of content, exactly as Coulomb and Newton give them (electric / gravity = rho_A rho_B, and with equal and opposite charges rho_e / rho_p = m_p / m_e), so they are an identity of the form and not a result of the law; a result worth publishing would be the number 1836 or the scale 10^36 derived from the law, and neither is. On the queue: "4, fine": no recorded-only item is queued. On the strong force: "1, fine, as long as it enters the whole of the laws; they can also escape in a collision, no?" DECIDED: the strong force enters as a generic column, sigma, a second density per unit of content in the same one coupling (M_A (rho_A rho_B - sigma_A sigma_B - 1) x flow, rho and sigma treated alike, no special case for any family), on a family of rays of its own. On the range, recorded for the physicist's design, flagged: a ray removed before the border is a one-way event, and by the owner's rule the click is the only one-way border, so the removal must be booked as a click (an escaped line in the ledger, as the face detector books an escape). Two forms: (a) a declared lifetime, a family key: the ray is booked as escaped when its age reaches L, local and deterministic from its own record, a range of L Links for every crowd, the analogue of the mediator's lifetime that gives Yukawa's range in nature; (b) the owner's question, an escape at a collision: a sigma ray that meets another ray in flight is booked as escaped instead of permuted, no new key, but a new rule (today two rays that meet are permuted by the bijective table and there is no click between rays), and the range then depends on the crowd: the density of a fan falls as 1 / r^2, so a fraction of the sigma rays always survives to infinity and the range is a suppression, not a cut. The orchestrator recommends (a) as the generic key (any family may declare a lifetime; a strong family is a family with the attractive column and a lifetime) and asks the physicist to weigh (b) against it in the design before any code; the implementation waits for Bohr to land. The owner, later the same day: "That is, whom will the information about the lifetime at a Node serve?" The answer, flagged: no one at a Node, since nothing is kept there; a lifetime is a key of the family, in the tables like `phase_per_link` and `quantum`, and the age is on the ray's own record, so the one place that uses it is the walk of the one function, where the flight already reads the age against the direction's period: at the interval the age reaches L the ray is booked as escaped (the ledger's escaped line, as at an open face) and is gone from the GameBoard. Its effect is on the readers beyond L Links, who then receive no sigma ray, which is the range; a family without the key lives forever, as every family does today.

**DECIDED: every experiment shows its results on a page with the GameBoard drawn (the owner, 2026-09-20).** The owner's words, translated: "Add to every experiment that is made that it shows results in HTML: on the GameBoard a detector with an icon, a star with an icon on the board, so that one understands exactly what is tested; and it states why it was tested and what the conclusion is." Recorded as the page contract of every research run (skills/simulation-runner, skills/boss-orchestrator): the GameBoard of the world drawn with an icon and a name for each thing on it (the detector's set, the star or source, the lamps, the walls, the probes) and a legend; "why it was tested" in the owner's words with the expectation written before the run; the moving picture inside the page when there is one; the readings against the expectation; "the conclusion" in plain words. The series in flight (G the Hubble diagram, H Bohr's lines) deliver their pages under this contract; the pages of A10 and of the prototypes stay as they are.

**The charge per unit of content landed, with B1, B3, the `wave` default and the refusal of face names (2026-09-20, the implementation).** A family's `charge` is rho, an integer c or a pair [n, d] (d >= 1; a paid family's rho is 0); a per-event charge is refused; a measured event's charge is the reduced pair rho x content; the record of a ray has seven fields (Node, direction, age, phase, number, amount, content) and no charge or mass column. The push is one product in one place: for a group of a free family's rays with label moment V read by A, push = -M_A V, and when both rho are nonzero, plus sign x by_clock(age_A, |V n_A n_B M_A|, d_A d_B); a paid ray's push is V. Series 7 replayed on both engines: every read record, event, momentum and book equal (181 records per world, 185 in 7_pp_m4); series C 19 inside / 9 outside as registered; Bell 326 of 326, S = 2 and S' = 3/2. Implemented for D2, flagged for the owner: a re-emitted ray keeps its family and takes the re-emitter's number, so it pushes by its own family's rho and carries nothing of the re-emitter (the record has no factor; a family change on re-emission would be incoherent for a paid family); the test pins a re-emitted ray of rho 1/4 through a mirror of rho 1/5 pushing by 1/4. B1: the label's product is checked per row before it is formed, the refusal naming the Node, the amount, the content, u_d and the product. B3: the push reads the content the frame read, once, so the family order of a world changes no integer. The default reading of a detector is `wave`; a detector named as a face is refused. Full check green, 423 tests.

**"To show atoms I do not need a detector" (the owner, 2026-09-20).** The owner's words on the results page, translated: "But to show atoms I do not need a detector. A detector receives their radiation, maybe, if it comes out, or shoots a ray at them, as physicists do." Recorded as the reading of the page contract and of series H: the atom is shown on the GameBoard itself, the proton and the electron's set and the path the electron makes; a detector is drawn only where the world declares one, and it has two uses, both the physicists' own: it receives what comes out of the atom (the rays the electron releases, which in this law carry its phase and no content, so the orbit does not decay), or it reads a beam shot at the atom by a lamp (absorption or scattering: the electron's table takes or passes the beam's rays by its threshold and phase window, and the detector behind reads what passed). The orchestrator's note, flagged: the second use is the natural place for absorption lines, since the phase window of the electron's table is a coherence condition between the beam's phase and the electron's own; it is added to series H as a second part if the budget allows, otherwise registered as the next step.

**DECIDED: the GameBoard is deterministic; the detector's world is not, by the uncertainty in the detector's world (the owner, 2026-09-20).** The owner's words, translated: "Record in Highlights if it is not there: the GameBoard is deterministic. The detector's world is not, because of the uncertainty principle in the detector's world. That is correct, no?" Recorded as the standing statement, with the orchestrator's reading of why it is correct under the law, flagged: on the GameBoard the interval is a function of the state, the flight table, the collision table and the tables of the measured events are fixed, there is no draw, no register and no remainder at a Node (the draw left the board on 2026-09-18), and the interval is a bijection up to the click, so the same GameBoard always gives the same next GameBoard, and the past can be recovered. A detector is a set of Nodes with one record: the record is a sum over the set and over the arrivals, so it does not hold which Node clicked or which ray, and many GameBoards give the same record; the detector's world is what the records say, and from the records alone the next record is not determined, because what determines it is on the GameBoard and not in the record. That is the uncertainty in the detector's world: not a draw of nature, but the information a set's record does not carry; the uncertainty principle of A10 (a width times a momentum spread bounded below) is its measured form, and the phase a set takes after a click, the pointer's, is what the detector's world calls the quantum state's draw. The earlier statements of this document that "only a detector generates a draw" are read the same way: the draw is in the detector's world, the GameBoard has none.

**The re-emitted ray keeps its family: "by the board's principle of preserving information this comes out by itself, no?" (the owner, 2026-09-20).** Confirmed and recorded. A ray's record is what the ray is (Node, direction, age, phase, number, amount, content; the family is its identity and its rho is a key of the family), and a measured event's table can only read, measure, re-release or pass it: the mirror turns the ray, it does not rewrite it. There is no column for a factor, the books balance per family (a family change at the mirror would destroy content in one family's line and create it in another's), and the tables come from the keys, so a re-emitted ray pushes by its own family's rho and carries nothing of the mirror; no rule had to be added. The one field that changes at a re-emission is the number, which becomes the mirror's, so that the mirror does not read its own re-emissions under the one reading set; that change happens at a click, the one-way border, where the law allows it.

**Why a proton and a neutron bind on the GameBoard today (the owner's question, 2026-09-20; the orchestrator's answer, flagged).** The one coupling is M_A (rho_A rho_B - 1) x flow; the neutron's rho is 0, so between a proton and a neutron only the -1 remains, a pure attraction in proportion to the reader's content and the other's flow (M / r^2), the same term that is gravity between every pair of families. It holds them only because the declared scale of the charge is small (with rho_p = 1 the electric push between two protons exactly cancels this term); at nature's scale (about 10^18 per unit of content in label units) the term is negligible and the pair would not bind without the sigma column, which is why the strong force is in design. The mechanics of the binding at one Link: each interval the two push toward each other, a step onto a Node that holds a measured event is refused, nothing is lost and nothing pushes back, so they stay at one Link. Found in the same reading, a defect for the strong-force design: at the refused step the momentum label stays and keeps growing inward every interval, without bound until the integer bound refuses the run, because the third law acts only through the rays and not at the contact; a contact rule is needed (the refused step handing its momentum component to the occupant, or the occupant reading the arriving body as a click of its table, or a repulsive core in sigma at zero distance), sent to the physicist's design of the strong force, together with the electron of width 3 reaching the proton.

**Alpha on the GameBoard (the owner, 2026-09-20: "these are good statements that explain alpha; explain alpha again"; the orchestrator's reading, flagged, sent to the strong-force design as expectations).** The alpha particle, two protons and two neutrons: between a proton and a neutron and between two neutrons only sigma pulls (at one Link, within the range L); between the two protons sigma pulls and the electric rho_p^2 pushes, net attraction at one Link only if sigma^2 > rho_p^2 - 1 and repulsion alone beyond L. On the cubic GameBoard the best cluster of four is the 2 x 2 square p n / n p: every proton touches two neutrons (four sigma bonds) and the two protons sit on the diagonal at sqrt 2, where the electric push is half its one-Link value and sigma reaches only if L >= 2; a corner or a line gives three bonds. Saturation comes from the range: a fifth nucleon on the square's side finds one or two bonds against the electric push of two protons, so He-5 and Li-5 should be unbound in the model as in nature. Alpha decay: in a nucleus of Z protons a surface alpha cluster is held by sigma through its L-neighbours only while the electric push of the other Z - 2 protons reaches it at 1 / r^2; when the push exceeds the hold the cluster is pushed out as one, since inside it sigma dominates; the GameBoard is deterministic and has no tunnelling, so a nucleus either holds or decays, and the only source of a spread of decay times in this law is the fan's grain, to be measured in series I. The mass defect is absent: a free ray carries no content and the alpha's content reads exactly 2 x 1836 + 2 x 1839. If "alpha" meant the fine-structure constant: it is the declared strength of the electric column, rho, and the law does not derive it.

**"Information to the detector about a Node is a ray the detector sends, which returns to it, and then it measures again?" (the owner, 2026-09-20).** Confirmed under the law and recorded. A detector is a set of Nodes with one record and reads only what arrives at its Nodes; there is no other channel, since the one reading set of every measured event is "everything at the Node but the reader's own number". So a detector learns of a distant Node in two ways only: from the rays the thing there releases by itself (its field or its radiation), or from a ray sent toward it (by the detector's own lamp or a lamp beside it) that the thing there re-releases or passes, read when it comes back or comes through. The returning ray is a new click at the set: a different ray (the re-release keeps its family, takes the mirror's number and starts its age at 0), read as amount, direction, age and phase, and the set takes the pointer's phase after the click. What the detector knows of the Node is exactly that: the direction its heading, the distance its age (the flight time, the age reading), the thing's phase and content in what came back; not which Node of the set clicked. And the sending is never free or passive: a paid unit costs the sender quantum x s (E = h f), and when the thing at the Node measures it, the unit's momentum pushes it, so looking at a thing moves it by the label of the ray used; the uncertainty of the detector's world (A10) is the measured form of this. The owner, going on: "So is information about a Node the detector sees still needed?" No: a detector declares only its own Nodes, its threshold, its reading and its window, and needs nothing of any other Node; "seeing" a Node is the arrival of rays from it. The host's diagnostics that read the dense arrays of the GameBoard (the shell readings of the tools) are not a detector and prove nothing physical. The owner, going on: "So why does it need information about the ray it sent?" It does not, and the law keeps none: a measured event's record is its own age, phase, momentum, content and owed count, with no memory of what it released; everything the detector learns is on the returning ray, whose age is the flight time since the re-release (a re-emission starts the age at 0, so the age is the one-way distance from the mirror), whose phase is the mirror's, and whose number is the mirror's, which is why the sender reads it at all under the one reading set (it never reads a ray of its own number). What the sending left on the sender is only its cost, quantum x s of content, in its own books.

**DECIDED: two kinds of readings; "in reality there is no such thing" (the owner, 2026-09-20).** Told that the tools' shell readings read the dense arrays of the GameBoard directly, the owner: "In reality there is no such thing." Recorded as the rule of the register and of every readings tool: every registered number is labelled a detector reading (the record of a detector's set or of a measured event in the world: a probe's read, a click, a pointer, an owed count; the only kind of reading reality has) or a GameBoard reading (the host's view of the deterministic board: a ray's position, the count or flow at a Node, a body's steps, the shell means, the books; it exists for us, not in reality). A comparison with nature uses detector readings only; a GameBoard reading describes the mechanism, checks the books or draws the picture, as the GIF of the board does. The engine's `shell_readings` and `cube_flux` are GameBoard readings and are named so; series C's far-field ripple (world 5) was registered from them and is marked a GameBoard reading until it is re-run with probes on the shells, as series E already reads its shells (probes of content 1 with `pass`). The series in flight (G, H) label their lines from the start.

**"And there is no ray, there is only an event" (the owner, 2026-09-20).** Said on the example of the lifetime, and recorded as the reading rule of every sentence in this document and in the code that says "ray": the one thing on the GameBoard is an event, a place, an interval and a record; what is called a ray, the `NatureBeam`, is the record of an event in transit, and "the ray flies" means that the event at a Node makes, by the flight table, the event at the next Node one Link on with the same record and the age one more; nothing persists as a thing beyond that. So a lifetime L is the family's rule that the event whose age reaches L makes no next event but an escape click in the ledger; a collision is several events at one Node in one interval, and the table says which events they make next; a measured event is an event that stays at its Node and makes events by its table; and a detector's record is a sum over the events that arrived at its set. The name NatureBeam stays, as the owner named it, for the record of the event in transit.

**DECIDED: the experimenter's skill; "the GameBoard is not measurable by a human" (the owner, 2026-09-20).** The owner's words, translated: "Let there be a special skill for the agent that makes real experiments, who always measures behind a detector or at an external thing. The GameBoard is not measurable by a human." Done as skills/experimenter/SKILL.md, the skill of every research run from this day: the question and the expectation first, a world that declares every thing a reading needs (detectors as sets, probes with `pass` on whole shells, lamps, stars), headless runs with the books balanced, a readings tool that reads the engine's functions and prints the kind of every line, the register entry with expected against measured and the kind of each reading, and the page with the GameBoard drawn; never a change to `src/`, never a GameBoard reading compared with nature, never a tuned world. Boss hands every experiment to an agent under this skill; the series in flight (G, H) were briefed to its rules before it was written.

**DECIDED: the strong force's range is a lifetime, L (the owner, 2026-09-20, "excellent, go for it").** After the explanation in events (an event never splits; L stops the reach, not a split: without it a strong event would go on forever and the force would fall as 1 / r^2 like gravity, with it every strong event ends after L Links and the force exists up to L and not beyond), the owner chose (a): a family key, the lifetime L, counted on the event's own age, which advances only while the event moves (a parked event does not age, a turn keeps the age, a re-release starts at 0), so L is a range of L Links; the event whose age reaches L makes no next event but an escape click in the ledger, as at an open face; a family without the key lives forever, as every family does today. Option (b), the escape at a collision, is recorded as considered and not chosen. The physicist's design (scratchpad/strong/DESIGN.md) closes on (a); the implementation follows it, with sigma the generic second column, the contact rule, the model identity, the tests and the migration note, and series I the nucleus registers the deuteron, the alpha square and He-5.

**"So L is like the clock but different: the clock slows the splitting and L decays it" (the owner, 2026-09-20).** Recorded with the reading, flagged: the two are the same primitive, an age on a record compared with a key of the family by whole division off the clock, with no memory at a Node; they differ in whose age. The clock is the counter of the thing at rest, the measured event: its age against K gives its turn and its releases (the "splitting" into the fan), the owed count read from the presence slows it in a crowd (the clock beside a mass), and a count of it will trigger a decay in time (the weak force's family change, when it comes). L is the counter of the thing in motion, the event in transit: its age against L ends it after L Links, blind to the crowd, so the fan reaches L and no further (the strong force's range). The clock is what a detector reads as a rate (E = h f, the redshift); L is what a detector reads as a range.

**DECIDED: the weak force is arranged in the world's terms, and the law is asked what else it predicts (the owner, 2026-09-20).** After the table of the weak force's things in the law's language (the W and Z as a weak family with the lifetime L of 1 or 0; the neutrino as a free family that every table passes and measures only within a narrow phase window, a cross-section without a draw; beta decay as a table entry of a measured event that turns it into another family and releases the rest, the books moving the content between the families' lines and the charge conserved; the half-life a detector reading over many decays in different crowds; parity violation the one thing the law lacks, a family with a hand under the 48 symmetries), the owner: "Arrange the weak force according to our world, and check whether we predict more things." Sent to the physicist as a read-only design: the weak force's keys, tables and conservation in the law's terms with a model identity, and a list of the law's own predictions beyond what it was built to reproduce, each with the reading that would test it and its status against what is observed. The owner, going on: "Is L a scalar or a vector?" A scalar: the age counts intervals of motion and the flight table gives every direction the same speed, 1 / sqrt 3 Links per interval, so L intervals are L / sqrt 3 Euclidean Links in every direction, a sphere and not a box; a vector L is refused. And: "Can the weak be unified with the strong?" In the law, structurally, yes: both are a family of events with a lifetime and columns in the one coupling, and what differs is what the reader's table does with the click, a push by the columns (the strong force, `measure`) or a transformation (the weak force, the new entry); a W-like family may do both. What nature's electroweak unification has and this does not (a broken symmetry, the scale set by the W and Z masses) and the hand are sent to the physicist to state plainly.

**DECIDED: "Our laws are on the GameBoard; in the detector one sees other laws" (the owner, 2026-09-20).** Said on the unification of the weak and the strong force, and recorded as the standing principle over "on the board a ray, in the world a wave": the laws of this model are the GameBoard's, the generic rules (families with their keys: the columns per unit of content, a lifetime, the phase per Link, the quantum; the tables from the keys; the flight and collision tables; the click), and the laws of nature (Newton, Einstein, Bohr, Yukawa's range, Hubble, the electroweak scale and its broken symmetry) are laws of the detector's world, what detectors read. So the GameBoard is never asked to carry the form of a law of nature, a comparison with nature is always between detector readings and nature's laws and never between the GameBoard's rules and nature's laws, and a question such as "are the weak and the strong force one" has a GameBoard answer (one mechanism, different keys) and a detector answer (what the readings show at each scale), which need not be the same. Put into the physics reviewer's skill.

**DECIDED: one mechanism for all the laws on the GameBoard (the owner, 2026-09-20: "maybe one mechanism for all the laws on the board?"; "just check that it is correct and that it works").** Recorded as the form of the law: an event with a record; a family with keys; a table derived from the keys; one interval function (the flight, the collision, the click). All the physics is in the world file and the engine branches on no force's name. What it takes to be true to the letter, to be verified before it is built: (1) the one coupling as a signed inner product over the columns a family declares per unit of content, push = M_A x (sum over the columns c of epsilon_c x c_A x c_B) x flow, each column's rational part taken by whole division off the clock with its own denominators as the electric column is today, and gravity the column every family has with the value 1 and the sign minus, so that M_A (rho_A rho_B - 1) x flow is the case of two columns and the strong force is a third column with the sign minus, not a term written in the code; a column's sign, plus for like values pushing apart and minus for like values pulling together, is a key and not a formula; (2) the lifetime L a key of any family, absent for ever-living families, the range of every force; (3) the table entries generic: read, measure, rerelease, pass and the transformation, so the weak force is a short-lived family with the transformation entry. The condition the owner set: correctness and working, meaning every registered integer reproduced bit for bit by the inner-product form (series C and 7, Bell, D, E), the bounds within 2^62 - 1 for the sum of the columns, and the third law by the rays unchanged; the mathematician verifies the form before the implementation, and the implementation of the strong force builds the generic columns instead of a special column.

**"And the entities seen behind a detector, the electron, the neutron, the quark: how are they unified on the GameBoard?" (the owner, 2026-09-20; the orchestrator's answer, flagged).** On the GameBoard an entity is a family, one row of keys: the content per unit (the mass), the columns per unit of content with their signs (electric rho, strong sigma, and any other), the lifetime L, the phase per Link (its rate), the quantum (paid or free) and its table (what it does with every other family's events: read, measure, rerelease, pass, and the transformation to come). The electron, the proton, the neutron, the photon, the neutrino and the W are rows of that one table and the engine does not know which is which. A composite entity is a set of measured events bound by a column with a lifetime (the nucleus by sigma, the atom by rho) and seen as one by a detector whose set covers it, by the owner's principle of sensitivity ("a detector measuring three Nodes sees one electron"); the same mechanism at every level, and the entity a detector names depends on the size of its set. Quarks are not modelled: the nucleon is a family. If they are wanted, the law already has their two oddities for free: the columns are rational pairs, so the charges 2/3 and -1/3 are ordinary values, and a nucleon would be three measured events of the families u and d bound by sigma at adjacent Nodes with the sum of their columns read by any detector whose set covers the three (the proton 2/3 + 2/3 - 1/3 = 1); what nature calls confinement is then a statement of the detector's world (no detector at the scale of a nucleon resolves one of the three), not a rule of the board. Recorded as a series after the strong force lands, if the owner wants it.

**Bohr behind the detector landed: the body on a set, the turn by momentum, series H (2026-09-20, the implementation and the run).** A measured event may be a body on a set of Nodes with one record (the key `span`, odd extents about its position; a set of one Node is bit-identical to today; the reading, the clock, the threshold and the push over the set's Nodes; the release split whole over the set; the step moves the set as one; a ray arriving at any Node of the set meets the body). The turn by momentum (the key `action`, h, and the declaration `phase_by_momentum` on the body; the hypothesis `bohr-v1` in the run's record): at every Link the body steps its phase turns by floor((k + 1) |p| N / h) - floor(k |p| N / h) with k the Links stepped before, derived from the age by the step rule, no register; the board unchanged with and without the keys (tests (a) to (f), 458 tests green). Series H, seven hydrogen worlds (the proton fixed, the electron a body of three Nodes at r = 2 to 16 with the tangential momentum, h fixed so that j = 2 at r = 8, the four open faces the `wave` detectors that receive the electron's rays), the expectation written first (closure 4 p r = j h on the lattice; the coherent record of the faces growing as the square of the turns where j is whole): no orbit closed within r / 4 at any radius; at r = 8 the electron's own reads gave 1.02 of the derived flux and two turns at the mean radius, then the whole-kick lumps and the close pass threw it out; the coherence C(2) = 0.84 against 1.0 expected, outside; Bohr's lines were not read, neither for nor against, and nothing was tuned.
